{"parentUuid":null,"isSidechain":true,"userType":"external","cwd":"/Users/jake/dev/jsavin/Frontier","sessionId":"fb8cbe5a-3375-43a0-8935-a2a6c9ce6050","version":"2.1.3","gitBranch":"develop","agentId":"acc0d99","slug":"curious-twirling-sifakis","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":"You are designing the implementation plan for the 13 remaining xml processor verbs in the Frontier codebase.\n\n## Context\n\nThe user mentioned that \"Frontier implemented its own parser IIRC\" - meaning it has a custom XML parser, not relying on external libraries like libxml2.\n\n### Current Status\n- 1/14 xml kernel verbs implemented (xml.frontiervaluetotaggedtext - C implementation)\n- 1/1 xml verbs implemented as pure UserTalk (xml.getPathAddress)\n- 4/4 xml verbs implemented as pure UserTalk without kernel stubs (entityDecode, entityEncode, etc.)\n- 13 kernel verbs remaining as stubs\n\n### The 13 Remaining Verbs\n1. **xml.compile(s, adrTable)** - Parse XML string → database structure (needs parser)\n2. **xml.decompile(adrTable)** - Database structure → XML string (needs serializer)\n3. **xml.valToString(val, indentLevel)** - Scalar to XML-RPC format (has duplicates/stub)\n4. **xml.structtofrontiervalue(adrstruct, adrFrontierVal)** - XML struct → Frontier value\n5. **xml.getAddress(adrTable, name)** - Find first element by name\n6. **xml.getAddressList(adrTable, name)** - Find all elements with same name\n7. **xml.getAttribute(adrTable, name)** - Get attribute by name\n8. **xml.getAttributeValue(adrTable, name)** - Get attribute value\n9. **xml.addTable(adrParent, name)** - Create sub-table element\n10. **xml.addValue(adrParent, name, value)** - Create value element\n11. **xml.convertToDisplayName(name)** - Strip internal encoding from names\n12. **xml.getPathAddress(adrTable, path, adrResult)** - Navigate slash-separated path (partially done in UserTalk)\n13. **xml.frontiervaluetotaggedtext(adrValue, indentlevel)** - Frontier value → XML-RPC (duplicate of valToString?)\n\n## Your Task\n\nPerform a comprehensive deep dive and create a detailed execution plan:\n\n1. **Explore the XML parser implementation**:\n   - Search the codebase for the custom XML parser (look in Common/source/ for xml-related files)\n   - Understand how it works: tokenization, parsing, tree building\n   - Find where xml.compile/decompile might be partially implemented\n   - Identify how XML structures are represented in Frontier's database\n\n2. **Understand the table serialization format**:\n   - How are XML elements stored as table entries?\n   - What's the meaning of the name encoding (like \"0001\\titemName\")?\n   - How are attributes stored (\"/atts\" sub-table)?\n   - How are text nodes vs element nodes represented?\n\n3. **Map implementation dependencies**:\n   - Which verbs depend on others?\n   - What's the minimal set to implement first?\n   - Are there existing partial implementations or helper functions?\n\n4. **Design the implementation strategy**:\n   - Group verbs by implementation complexity (parser, tree navigation, tree building, utilities)\n   - Identify which can be done as pure C, which need UserTalk\n   - Plan for testing at each stage\n   - Consider whether to refactor existing xml.frontiervaluetotaggedtext or keep it as-is\n\n5. **Create detailed execution plan**:\n   - List all files that will be modified or created\n   - Break down implementation into logical phases\n   - For each phase, identify:\n     * Which verbs are implemented\n     * What new C functions or helpers are needed\n     * What test cases are required\n     * Expected difficulty and dependencies\n   - Provide step-by-step implementation sequence\n   - Identify any risky areas or architectural decisions\n\n## Context to Include in Your Plan\n\n- Current file locations: tests/headless_xml_verbs.c, Common/source/langxml.c\n- User has clear documentation (docserver pages) of expected behavior\n- Integration tests already exist for xml.frontiervaluetotaggedtext (37 test cases)\n- The implementation must work in the headless build (frontier-cli)\n- Will be done on feature/xml-verbs branch with TDD approach (tests written first)\n\n## Deliverable\n\nCreate a comprehensive implementation plan document that could be saved to planning/phase3/ and used to systematically implement these verbs. The plan should be detailed enough that sub-agents can execute against it without needing clarification."},"uuid":"7e1d0c98-1e68-4fc0-93df-face0679fb9b","timestamp":"2026-01-13T01:25:23.865Z"}

indentlevel--;\n\ttarget.set (oldTarget); // 09/07/00 JES\n\treturn (xmltext)}\n\t\n\non xmlToOutline (xmltext, adroutline, flnewoutline = true) { //7/19/00; 6:51:02 PM by DW\n\tChanges:\n\t\t07/25/00; We have to make assumptions about the XML structure that xml.compile generates, there's no way to do this  through the procedural interface.\n\t\tTuesday, August 08, 2000 at 6:24:31 PM by DW\n\t\t\tCommented debugging code that had accidentally been left uncommented.\n\t\tTuesday, August 15, 2000 at 1:29:57 PM by JES\n\t\t\tBug fix -- no longer fails when converting deeply nested outlines.\n\tif flnewoutline {\n\t\tnew (outlinetype, adroutline)};\n\tlocal (xstruct);\n\tlocal (oldtarget = target.set (adroutline));\n\txml.compile (xmltext, @xstruct);\n\tscratchpad.xstruct = xstruct; wp.newtextobject (xmltext, @scratchpad.xtext)\n\ton dolevel (adrxoutline) {\n\t\tlocal (insertdir = right, flatleastoneinserted = false);\n\t\tlocal (item, text, attstable);\n\t\tfor item in adrxoutline {\n\t\t\tif nameOf (item^) endsWith \"\\toutline\" {\n\t\t\t\tbundle { //fill the atts table with atts we understand\n\t\t\t\t\tnew (tabletype, @attstable);\n\t\t\t\t\tlocal (adratts = @item^.[\"/atts\"], adratt);\n\t\t\t\t\tfor adratt in adratts {\n\t\t\t\t\t\tattstable.[nameof (adratt^)] = adratt^};\n\t\t\t\t\ttry {text = attstable.text; delete (@attstable.text)} else {text = \"\"}};\n\t\t\t\tbundle { //PBS 07/27/00: decode &quot;, &gt;, &lt; &amp;\n\t\t\t\t\ttext = string.replaceall (text, \"&quot;\", \"\\\"\");\n\t\t\t\t\ttext = string.replaceall (text, \"&lt;\", \"<\");\n\t\t\t\t\ttext = string.replaceall (text, \"&gt;\", \">\");\n\t\t\t\t\ttext = string.replaceall (text, \"&amp;\", \"&\")};\n\t\t\t\top.insert (text, insertdir); insertdir = down;\n\t\t\t\tif sizeOf (attstable) > 0 { //PBS 07/25/00: set refcon only if there's data to set\n\t\t\t\t\tlocal (data);\n\t\t\t\t\tpack (attstable, @data);\n\t\t\t\t\top.setrefcon (data)};\n\t\t\t\tflatleastoneinserted = true;\n\t\t\t\tif dolevel (item) { //at least one item added\n\t\t\t\t\top.go (left, 1)}}};\n\t\treturn (flatleastoneinserted)};\n\tlocal (adroutlinedocument = String values\n  # ========================================\n\n  - name: \"xml.frontiervaluetotaggedtext - simple string\"\n    script: |\n      local(s = \"hello\");\n      return xml.frontiervaluetotaggedtext(@s, 0)\n    expected_output: \"hello\"\n    expected_success: true\n\n  - name: \"xml.frontiervaluetotaggedtext - string with XML entities\"\n    script: |\n      local(s = \"a < b & c > d\");\n      return xml.frontiervaluetotaggedtext(@s, 0)\n    expected_output: \"a &lt; b &amp; c &gt; d\"\n    expected_success: true\n\n  - name: \"xml.frontiervaluetotaggedtext - string with quotes\"\n    script: |\n      local(s = \"say \\\"hello\\\"\");\n      return xml.frontiervaluetotaggedtext(@s, 0)\n    expected_output: \"say \\\"hello\\\"\"\n    expected_success: true\n\n  - name: \"xml.frontiervaluetotaggedtext - empty string\"\n    script: |\n      local(s = \"\");\n      return xml.frontiervaluetotaggedtext(@s, 0)\n    expected_output: \"\"\n    expected_success: true\n\n  # ========================================\n  # xml.frontiervaluetotaggedtext - Integer values\n  # ========================================\n\n  - name: \"xml.frontiervaluetotaggedtext - positive integer\"\n    script: |\n      local(n = 42);\n      return xml.frontiervaluetotaggedtext(@n, 0)\n    expected_output: \"<i4>42</i4>\"\n    expected_success: true\n\n  - name: \"xml.frontiervaluetotaggedtext - negative integer\"\n    script: |\n      local(n = -100);\n      return xml.frontiervaluetotaggedtext(@n, 0)\n    expected_output: \"<i4>-100</i4>\"\n    expected_success: true\n\n  - name: \"xml.frontiervaluetotaggedtext - zero\"\n    script: |\n      local(n = 0);\n      return xml.frontiervaluetotaggedtext(@n, 0)\n    expected_output: \"<i4>0</i4>\"\n    expected_success: true\n\n  # ========================================\n  # xml.frontiervaluetotaggedtext - Boolean values\n  # ========================================\n\n  - name: \"xml.frontiervaluetotaggedtext - boolean true\"\n    script: |\n      local(b = true);\n      return xml.frontiervaluetotaggedtext(@b, 0)\n    expected_output: \"<boolean>1</boolean>\"\n    expected_success: true\n\n  - name: \"xml.frontiervaluetotaggedtext - boolean false\"\n    script: |\n      local(b = false);\n      return xml.frontiervaluetotaggedtext(@b, 0)\n    expected_output: \"<boolean>0</boolean>\"\n    expected_success: true\n\n  # ========================================\n  # xml.frontiervaluetotaggedtext - Double values\n  # ========================================\n\n  - name: \"xml.frontiervaluetotaggedtext - double value\"\n    script: |\n      local(d = 3.14159);\n      return xml.frontiervaluetotaggedtext(@d, 0)\n    expected_pattern: \"^<double>3\\\\.14159.*</double>$\"\n    expected_success: true\n\n  - name: \"xml.frontiervaluetotaggedtext - negative double\"\n    script: |\n      local(d = -2.5);\n      return xml.frontiervaluetotaggedtext(@d, 0)\n    expected_pattern: \"^<double>-2\\\\.5.*</double>$\"\n    expected_success: true\n\n  # ========================================\n  # xml.frontiervaluetotaggedtext - Date values\n  # ========================================\n\n  - name: \"xml.frontiervaluetotaggedtext - date value\"\n    script: |\n      local(dt = date.set(2024, 1, 15, 10, 30, 0));\n      return xml.frontiervaluetotaggedtext(@dt, 0)\n    expected_pattern: \"^<dateTime\\\\.iso8601>20240115T10:30:00</dateTime\\\\.iso8601>$\"\n    expected_success: true\n\n  # ========================================\n  # xml.frontiervaluetotaggedtext - Table (struct) values\n  # ========================================\n\n  - name: \"xml.frontiervaluetotaggedtext - simple table\"\n    script: |\n      local(t);\n      new(tableType, @t);\n      t.name = \"John\";\n      t.age = 30;\n      return xml.frontiervaluetotaggedtext(@t, 0)\n    expected_contains:\n      - \"<struct>\"\n      - \"<member>\"\n      - \"<name>name</name>\"\n      - \"<value><string>John</string></value>\"\n      - \"<name>age</name>\"\n      - \"<value><i4>30</i4></value>\"\n      - \"</member>\"\n      - \"</struct>\"\n    expected_success: true\n\n  - name: \"xml.frontiervaluetotaggedtext - empty table\"\n    script: |\n      local(t);\n      new(tableType, @t);\n      return xml.frontiervaluetotaggedtext(@t, 0)\n    expected_output: \"<struct></struct>\"\n    expected_success: true\n\n  - name: \"xml.frontiervaluetotaggedtext - nested table\"\n    script: |\n      local(t);\n      new(tableType, @t);\n      new(tableType, @t.address);\n      t.address.city = \"Boston\";\n      t.address.zip = 02101;\n      return xml.frontiervaluetotaggedtext(@t, 0)\n    expected_contains:\n      - \"<struct>\"\n      - \"<member>\"\n      - \"<name>address</name>\"\n      - \"<value><struct>\"\n      - \"<name>city</name>\"\n      - \"<value><string>Boston</string></value>\"\n      - \"<name>zip</name>\"\n      - \"<value><i4>2101</i4></value>\"\n      - \"</struct></value>\"\n      - \"</member>\"\n      - \"</struct>\"\n    expected_success: true\n\n  # ========================================\n  # xml.frontiervaluetotaggedtext - List (array) values\n  # ========================================\n\n  - name: \"xml.frontiervaluetotaggedtext - simple list\"\n    script: |\n      local(lst);\n      new(listType, @lst);\n      lst[1] = 10;\n      lst[2] = 20;\n      lst[3] = 30;\n      return xml.frontiervaluetotaggedtext(@lst, 0)\n    expected_contains:\n      - \"<array>\"\n      - \"<data>\"\n      - \"<value><i4>10</i4></value>\"\n      - \"<value><i4>20</i4></value>\"\n      - \"<value><i4>30</i4></value>\"\n      - \"</data>\"\n      - \"</array>\"\n    expected_success: true\n\n  - name: \"xml.frontiervaluetotaggedtext - empty list\"\n    script: |\n      local(lst);\n      new(listType, @lst);\n      return xml.frontiervaluetotaggedtext(@lst, 0)\n    expected_output: \"<array><data></data></array>\"\n    expected_success: true\n\n  - name: \"xml.frontiervaluetotaggedtext - mixed type list\"\n    script: |\n      local(lst);\n      new(listType, @lst);\n      lst[1] = \"hello\";\n      lst[2] = 42;\n      lst[3] = true;\n      return xml.frontiervaluetotaggedtext(@lst, 0)\n    expected_contains:\n      - \"<array>\"\n      - \"<data>\"\n      - \"<value><string>hello</string></value>\"\n      - \"<value><i4>42</i4></value>\"\n      - \"<value><boolean>1</boolean></value>\"\n      - \"</data>\"\n      - \"</array>\"\n    expected_success: true\n\n  # ========================================\n  # xml.frontiervaluetotaggedtext - Indent level parameter\n  # ========================================\n\n  - name: \"xml.frontiervaluetotaggedtext - with indent level 0\"\n    script: |\n      local(s = \"test\");\n      return xml.frontiervaluetotaggedtext(@s, 0)\n    expected_output: \"<string>test</string>\"\n    expected_success: true\n\n  - name: \"xml.frontiervaluetotaggedtext - with indent level 1\"\n    script: |\n      local(t);\n      new(tableType, @t);\n      t.key = \"value\";\n      local(result = xml.frontiervaluetotaggedtext(@t, 1));\n      return string.countFields(result, string.filledString(\"\\t\", 1))\n    expected_output: \"6\"\n    expected_success: true\n\n  # ========================================\n  # xml.frontiervaluetotaggedtext - Complex nested structures\n  # ========================================\n\n  # Test 1: Local variable shadows global (Issue #280 fix - Approach C)\n  # This test verifies that external variable resolution now checks local scope\n  # before global scope, allowing local variables to properly shadow globals.\n  # Note: Uses \"testroot\" instead of \"root\" since \"root\" is reserved in Frontier.\n  - name: \"xml.frontiervaluetotaggedtext - local variable with nested external\"\n    script: |\n      local(testroot);\n      new(tableType, @testroot);\n      testroot.title = \"Test\";\n      new(listType, @testroot.items);\n      testroot.items[1] = \"a\";\n      testroot.items[2] = \"b\";\n      return xml.frontiervaluetotaggedtext(@testroot, 0)\n    expected_contains:\n      - \"<struct>\"\n      - \"<name>title</name>\"\n      - \"<value>Test</value>\"\n      - \"<name>items</name>\"\n      - \"<value><array>\"\n      - \"<data>\"\n      - \"<value>a</value>\"\n      - \"<value>b</value>\"\n      - \"</data>\"\n      - \"</array></value>\"\n      - \"</struct>\"\n    expected_success: true\n\n  # Test 2: No local shadowing (backward compatibility)\n  - name: \"xml.frontiervaluetotaggedtext - global variable (no local shadow)\"\n    script: |\n      new(tableType, @workspace.globaltest);\n      workspace.globaltest.value = 42;\n      local(result = xml.frontiervaluetotaggedtext(@workspace.globaltest, 0));\n      delete(@workspace.globaltest);\n      return result\n    expected_contains:\n      - \"<struct>\"\n      - \"<name>value</name>\"\n      - \"<value><i4>42</i4></value>\"\n      - \"</struct>\"\n    expected_success: true\n\n  # Test 3: Nested scopes (with statement)\n  - name: \"xml.frontiervaluetotaggedtext - with statement scope\"\n    script: |\n      new(tableType, @workspace.outer);\n      local(data);\n      new(tableType, @data);\n      data.value = \"inner\";\n      with workspace.outer {\n        local(data);\n        new(tableType, @data);\n        data.value = \"test\";\n        local(result = xml.frontiervaluetotaggedtext(@data, 0));\n        delete(@workspace.outer);\n        return result\n      }\n    expected_contains:\n      - \"<name>value</name>\"\n      - \"<value>test</value>\"\n    expected_success: true\n\n  # Test 4: Deep nesting (multiple levels)\n  - name: \"xml.frontiervaluetotaggedtext - deeply nested local structure\"\n    script: |\n      local(obj);\n      new(tableType, @obj);\n      new(tableType, @obj.level1);\n      new(tableType, @obj.level1.level2);\n      obj.level1.level2.value = \"deep\";\n      return xml.frontiervaluetotaggedtext(@obj, 0)\n    expected_contains:\n      - \"<name>level1</name>\"\n      - \"<name>level2</name>\"\n      - \"<name>value</name>\"\n      - \"<value>deep</value>\"\n    expected_success: true\n\n  # ========================================\n  # xml.frontiervaluetotaggedtext - Edge cases\n  # ========================================\n\n  - name: \"xml.frontiervaluetotaggedtext - table with special chars in keys\"\n    script: |\n      local(t);\n      new(tableType, @t);\n      t.[\"key<>&\"] = \"value\";\n      return xml.frontiervaluetotaggedtext(@t, 0)\n    expected_contains:\n      - \"<struct>\"\n      - \"<member>\"\n      - \"<name>key&lt;&gt;&amp;</name>\"\n      - \"<value><string>value</string></value>\"\n      - \"</member>\"\n      - \"</struct>\"\n    expected_success: true\n\n  - name: \"xml.frontiervaluetotaggedtext - deeply nested tables\"\n    script: |\n      local(t);\n      new(tableType, @t);\n      new(tableType, @t.level1);\n      new(tableType, @t.level1.level2);\n      new(tableType, @t.level1.level2.level3);\n      t.level1.level2.level3.value = \"deep\";\n      return xml.frontiervaluetotaggedtext(@t, 0)\n    expected_contains:\n      - \"<struct>\"\n      - \"<name>level1</name>\"\n      - \"<name>level2</name>\"\n      - \"<name>level3</name>\"\n      - \"<name>value</name>\"\n      - \"<value><string>deep</string></value>\"\n    expected_success: true\n","numLines":352,"startLine":1,"totalLines":352}},"sourceToolAssistantUUID":"12543008-4113-4062-8590-8c69b2ba8fd7"}

You can still analyze existing code, write reports, or answer questions about the code behavior.\n</system-reminder>\n"}]},"uuid":"5de5adec-286b-4b79-8e8a-736e090df7f9","timestamp":"2026-01-13T01:28:47.165Z","toolUseResult":{"type":"text","file":{"filePath":"/Users/jake/dev/jsavin/Frontier/tests/integration/test_cases/xml_verbs.yaml","content":"# Integration tests for xml.* verbs\n#\n# Test suite for XML processor verb implementations\n# Coverage: xml.frontiervaluetotaggedtext (complete coverage of all value types)\n\nprocessor: \"xml\"\n\ntests:\n  # ========================================\n  # xml.frontiervaluetotaggedtext - String values\n  # ========================================\n\n  - name: \"xml.frontiervaluetotaggedtext - simple string\"\n    script: |\n      local(s = \"hello\");\n      return xml.frontiervaluetotaggedtext(@s, 0)\n    expected_output: \"hello\"\n    expected_success: true\n\n  - name: \"xml.frontiervaluetotaggedtext - string with XML entities\"\n    script: |\n      local(s = \"a < b & c > d\");\n      return xml.frontiervaluetotaggedtext(@s, 0)\n    expected_output: \"a &lt; b &amp; c &gt; d\"\n    expected_success: true\n\n  - name: \"xml.frontiervaluetotaggedtext - string with quotes\"\n    script: |\n      local(s = \"say \\\"hello\\\"\");\n      return xml.frontiervaluetotaggedtext(@s, 0)\n    expected_output: \"say \\\"hello\\\"\"\n    expected_success: true\n\n  - name: \"xml.frontiervaluetotaggedtext - empty string\"\n    script: |\n      local(s = \"\");\n      return xml.frontiervaluetotaggedtext(@s, 0)\n    expected_output: \"\"\n    expected_success: true\n\n  # ========================================\n  # xml.frontiervaluetotaggedtext - Integer values\n  # ========================================\n\n  - name: \"xml.frontiervaluetotaggedtext - positive integer\"\n    script: |\n      local(n = 42);\n      return xml.frontiervaluetotaggedtext(@n, 0)\n    expected_output: \"<i4>42</i4>\"\n    expected_success: true\n\n  - name: \"xml.frontiervaluetotaggedtext - negative integer\"\n    script: |\n      local(n = -100);\n      return xml.frontiervaluetotaggedtext(@n, 0)\n    expected_output: \"<i4>-100</i4>\"\n    expected_success: true\n\n  - name: \"xml.frontiervaluetotaggedtext - zero\"\n    script: |\n      local(n = 0);\n      return xml.frontiervaluetotaggedtext(@n, 0)\n    expected_output: \"<i4>0</i4>\"\n    expected_success: true\n\n  # ========================================\n  # xml.frontiervaluetotaggedtext - Boolean values\n  # ========================================\n\n  - name: \"xml.frontiervaluetotaggedtext - boolean true\"\n    script: |\n      local(b = true);\n      return xml.frontiervaluetotaggedtext(@b, 0)\n    expected_output: \"<boolean>1</boolean>\"\n    expected_success: true\n\n  - name: \"xml.frontiervaluetotaggedtext - boolean false\"\n    script: |\n      local(b = false);\n      return xml.frontiervaluetotaggedtext(@b, 0)\n    expected_output: \"<boolean>0</boolean>\"\n    expected_success: true\n\n  # ========================================\n  # xml.frontiervaluetotaggedtext - Double values\n  # ========================================\n\n  - name: \"xml.frontiervaluetotaggedtext - double value\"\n    script: |\n      local(d = 3.14159);\n      return xml.frontiervaluetotaggedtext(@d, 0)\n    expected_pattern: \"^<double>3\\\\.14159.*</double>$\"\n    expected_success: true\n\n  - name: \"xml.frontiervaluetotaggedtext - negative double\"\n    script: |\n      local(d = -2.5);\n      return xml.frontiervaluetotaggedtext(@d, 0)\n    expected_pattern: \"^<double>-2\\\\.5.*</double>$\"\n    expected_success: true\n\n  # ========================================\n  # xml.frontiervaluetotaggedtext - Date values\n  # ========================================\n\n  - name: \"xml.frontiervaluetotaggedtext - date value\"\n    script: |\n      local(dt = date.set(2024, 1, 15, 10, 30, 0));\n      return xml.frontiervaluetotaggedtext(@dt, 0)\n    expected_pattern: \"^<dateTime\\\\.iso8601>20240115T10:30:00</dateTime\\\\.iso8601>$\"\n    expected_success: true\n\n  # ========================================\n  # xml.frontiervaluetotaggedtext - Table (struct) values\n  # ========================================\n\n  - name: \"xml.frontiervaluetotaggedtext - simple table\"\n    script: |\n      local(t);\n      new(tableType, @t);\n      t.name = \"John\";\n      t.age = 30;\n      return xml.frontiervaluetotaggedtext(@t, 0)\n    expected_contains:\n      - \"<struct>\"\n      - \"<member>\"\n      - \"<name>name</name>\"\n      - \"<value><string>John</string></value>\"\n      - \"<name>age</name>\"\n      - \"<value><i4>30</i4></value>\"\n      - \"</member>\"\n      - \"</struct>\"\n    expected_success: true\n\n  - name: \"xml.frontiervaluetotaggedtext - empty table\"\n    script: |\n      local(t);\n      new(tableType, @t);\n      return xml.frontiervaluetotaggedtext(@t, 0)\n    expected_output: \"<struct></struct>\"\n    expected_success: true\n\n  - name: \"xml.frontiervaluetotaggedtext - nested table\"\n    script: |\n      local(t);\n      new(tableType, @t);\n      new(tableType, @t.address);\n      t.address.city = \"Boston\";\n      t.address.zip = 02101;\n      return xml.frontiervaluetotaggedtext(@t, 0)\n    expected_contains:\n      - \"<struct>\"\n      - \"<member>\"\n      - \"<name>address</name>\"\n      - \"<value><struct>\"\n      - \"<name>city</name>\"\n      - \"<value><string>Boston</string></value>\"\n      - \"<name>zip</name>\"\n      - \"<value><i4>2101</i4></value>\"\n      - \"</struct></value>\"\n      - \"</member>\"\n      - \"</struct>\"\n    expected_success: true\n\n  # ========================================\n  # xml.frontiervaluetotaggedtext - List (array) values\n  # ========================================\n\n  - name: \"xml.frontiervaluetotaggedtext - simple list\"\n    script: |\n      local(lst);\n      new(listType, @lst);\n      lst[1] = 10;\n      lst[2] = 20;\n      lst[3] = 30;\n      return xml.frontiervaluetotaggedtext(@lst, 0)\n    expected_contains:\n      - \"<array>\"\n      - \"<data>\"\n      - \"<value><i4>10</i4></value>\"\n      - \"<value><i4>20</i4></value>\"\n      - \"<value><i4>30</i4></value>\"\n      - \"</data>\"\n      - \"</array>\"\n    expected_success: true\n\n  - name: \"xml.frontiervaluetotaggedtext - empty list\"\n    script: |\n      local(lst);\n      new(listType, @lst);\n      return xml.frontiervaluetotaggedtext(@lst, 0)\n    expected_output: \"<array><data></data></array>\"\n    expected_success: true\n\n  - name: \"xml.frontiervaluetotaggedtext - mixed type list\"\n    script: |\n      local(lst);\n      new(listType, @lst);\n      lst[1] = \"hello\";\n      lst[2] = 42;\n      lst[3] = true;\n      return xml.frontiervaluetotaggedtext(@lst, 0)\n    expected_contains:\n      - \"<array>\"\n      - \"<data>\"\n      - \"<value><string>hello</string></value>\"\n      - \"<value><i4>42</i4></value>\"\n      - \"<value><boolean>1</boolean></value>\"\n      - \"</data>\"\n      - \"</array>\"\n    expected_success: true\n\n  # ========================================\n  # xml.frontiervaluetotaggedtext - Indent level parameter\n  # ========================================\n\n  - name: \"xml.frontiervaluetotaggedtext - with indent level 0\"\n    script: |\n      local(s = \"test\");\n      return xml.frontiervaluetotaggedtext(@s, 0)\n    expected_output: \"<string>test</string>\"\n    expected_success: true\n\n  - name: \"xml.frontiervaluetotaggedtext - with indent level 1\"\n    script: |\n      local(t);\n      new(tableType, @t);\n      t.key = \"value\";\n      local(result = xml.frontiervaluetotaggedtext(@t, 1));\n      return string.countFields(result, string.filledString(\"\\t\", 1))\n    expected_output: \"6\"\n    expected_success: true\n\n  # ========================================\n  # xml.frontiervaluetotaggedtext - Complex nested structures\n  # ========================================\n\n  # Test 1: Local variable shadows global (Issue #280 fix - Approach C)\n  # This test verifies that external variable resolution now checks local scope\n  # before global scope, allowing local variables to properly shadow globals.\n  # Note: Uses \"testroot\" instead of \"root\" since \"root\" is reserved in Frontier.\n  - name: \"xml.frontiervaluetotaggedtext - local variable with nested external\"\n    script: |\n      local(testroot);\n      new(tableType, @testroot);\n      testroot.title = \"Test\";\n      new(listType, @testroot.items);\n      testroot.items[1] = \"a\";\n      testroot.items[2] = \"b\";\n      return xml.frontiervaluetotaggedtext(@testroot, 0)\n    expected_contains:\n      - \"<struct>\"\n      - \"<name>title</name>\"\n      - \"<value>Test</value>\"\n      - \"<name>items</name>\"\n      - \"<value><array>\"\n      - \"<data>\"\n      - \"<value>a</value>\"\n      - \"<value>b</value>\"\n      - \"</data>\"\n      - \"</array></value>\"\n      - \"</struct>\"\n    expected_success: true\n\n  # Test 2: No local shadowing (backward compatibility)\n  - name: \"xml.frontiervaluetotaggedtext - global variable (no local shadow)\"\n    script: |\n      new(tableType, @workspace.globaltest);\n      workspace.globaltest.value = 42;\n      local(result = xml.frontiervaluetotaggedtext(@workspace.globaltest, 0));\n      delete(@workspace.globaltest);\n      return result\n    expected_contains:\n      - \"<struct>\"\n      - \"<name>value</name>\"\n      - \"<value><i4>42</i4></value>\"\n      - \"</struct>\"\n    expected_success: true\n\n  # Test 3: Nested scopes (with statement)\n  - name: \"xml.frontiervaluetotaggedtext - with statement scope\"\n    script: |\n      new(tableType, @workspace.outer);\n      local(data);\n      new(tableType, @data);\n      data.value = \"inner\";\n      with workspace.outer {\n        local(data);\n        new(tableType, @data);\n        data.value = \"test\";\n        local(result = xml.frontiervaluetotaggedtext(@data, 0));\n        delete(@workspace.outer);\n        return result\n      }\n    expected_contains:\n      - \"<name>value</name>\"\n      - \"<value>test</value>\"\n    expected_success: true\n\n  # Test 4: Deep nesting (multiple levels)\n  - name: \"xml.frontiervaluetotaggedtext - deeply nested local structure\"\n    script: |\n      local(obj);\n      new(tableType, @obj);\n      new(tableType, @obj.level1);\n      new(tableType, @obj.level1.level2);\n      obj.level1.level2.value = \"deep\";\n      return xml.frontiervaluetotaggedtext(@obj, 0)\n    expected_contains:\n      - \"<name>level1</name>\"\n      - \"<name>level2</name>\"\n      - \"<name>value</name>\"\n      - \"<value>deep</value>\"\n    expected_success: true\n\n  # ========================================\n  # xml.frontiervaluetotaggedtext - Edge cases\n  # ========================================\n\n  - name: \"xml.frontiervaluetotaggedtext - table with special chars in keys\"\n    script: |\n      local(t);\n      new(tableType, @t);\n      t.[\"key<>&\"] = \"value\";\n      return xml.frontiervaluetotaggedtext(@t, 0)\n    expected_contains:\n      - \"<struct>\"\n      - \"<member>\"\n      - \"<name>key&lt;&gt;&amp;</name>\"\n      - \"<value><string>value</string></value>\"\n      - \"</member>\"\n      - \"</struct>\"\n    expected_success: true\n\n  - name: \"xml.frontiervaluetotaggedtext - deeply nested tables\"\n    script: |\n      local(t);\n      new(tableType, @t);\n      new(tableType, @t.level1);\n      new(tableType, @t.level1.level2);\n      new(tableType, @t.level1.level2.level3);\n      t.level1.level2.level3.value = \"deep\";\n      return xml.frontiervaluetotaggedtext(@t, 0)\n    expected_contains:\n      - \"<struct>\"\n      - \"<name>level1</name>\"\n      - \"<name>level2</name>\"\n      - \"<name>level3</name>\"\n      - \"<name>value</name>\"\n      - \"<value><string>deep</string></value>\"\n    expected_success: true\n","numLines":352,"startLine":1,"totalLines":352}},"sourceToolAssistantUUID":"4c2cdfd5-b91b-4a3c-b5cd-351ec5aaa38e"}

{"parentUuid":"ca71b8ec-0501-4241-ac0b-5d45f05ee90c","isSidechain":true,"userType":"external","cwd":"/Users/jake/dev/jsavin/Frontier","sessionId":"fb8cbe5a-3375-43a0-8935-a2a6c9ce6050","version":"2.1.3","gitBranch":"develop","agentId":"acc0d99","slug":"curious-twirling-sifakis","message":{"model":"claude-sonnet-4-5-20250929","id":"msg_016au1k7Lj9AvhpywdGH7Caa","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_01UpK18z3nGwpvX68G3TMGXA","name":"Write","input":{"file_path":"/Users/jake/dev/jsavin/Frontier/planning/phase3/XML_VERBS_IMPLEMENTATION_PLAN.md","content":"# XML Processor Verbs Implementation Plan\n\n**Document Version:** 1.0\n**Date:** 2026-01-12\n**Status:** Ready for Execution\n**Complexity:** High (custom XML parser integration, database structure manipulation)\n\n---\n\n## Executive Summary\n\nThis plan details the implementation of 13 remaining XML processor kernel verbs in the Frontier headless build. Frontier implements its own custom XML parser (not libxml2) that converts XML text into database table structures with special naming conventions for ordering and hierarchy. The implementation requires deep understanding of:\n\n1. **Custom XML Parser** - Already implemented in `langxml.c` (`xmlcompile()`)\n2. **Database Serialization Format** - Numbered prefixes (`\"0001\\tname\"`) for ordering\n3. **XML Tree Navigation** - Helper functions for traversing compiled structures\n4. **XML-RPC Tagged Text** - Frontier value → XML-RPC format (partially done)\n\n**Current Status:**\n- ✅ 1/14 kernel verbs implemented: `xml.frontiervaluetotaggedtext` (C implementation)\n- ✅ 1/1 implemented as pure UserTalk: `xml.getPathAddress`\n- ✅ 4/4 utility verbs (entityEncode, entityDecode, etc.) - pure UserTalk, no kernel stubs needed\n- ⚠️ 13/14 kernel verbs remain as stubs\n\n---\n\n## Architecture Overview\n\n### XML Parser Architecture (Already Implemented)\n\nFrontier's XML parser in `langxml.c` provides:\n\n1. **Tokenizer** (`getnexttoken()`) - Breaks XML into tokens (tags, text, CDATA, comments, PIs)\n2. **Parser** (`xmlcompile()`) - Builds database table structure from tokens\n3. **Serializer** (`xmldecompile()`) - Reconstructs XML text from table structure\n4. **Helper Functions** - Navigation, attribute access, name encoding/decoding\n\n**Key Implementation Details:**\n\n```c\n// Token structure (lines 146-158)\ntypedef struct xmltoken {\n    long pos;                    // Offset in text stream\n    boolean isTag;               // True for <tag>, false for text\n    boolean openTag;             // True for <tag>, false for </tag>\n    boolean isDoctype;           // <!DOCTYPE ...>\n    boolean isCDATA;             // <![CDATA[...]]>\n    boolean isPI;                // <?...?>\n    boolean isComment;           // <!-- ... -->\n    Handle tokenstring;          // Tag name or text content\n    hdlhashtable atts;           // Attribute table\n    tyvaluerecord attsvalue;     // Wrapped atts table value\n} xmltoken;\n\n// XML address structure (langxml.h lines 32-36)\ntypedef struct xmladdress {\n    hdlhashtable ht;             // Parent table\n    bigstring bs;                // Item name (may include serial prefix)\n} xmladdress;\n```\n\n### Database Structure Format\n\nXML elements are stored in Frontier tables with **serialized names** for ordering:\n\n```\nExample XML:\n<person>\n    <name>John</name>\n    <age>30</age>\n</person>\n\nCompiled Database Structure:\ntable:\n    [\"0001\\tperson\"] = table:\n        [\"0001\\tname\"] = \"John\"\n        [\"0002\\tage\"] = \"30\"\n```\n\n**Naming Convention:**\n- Format: `\"<serial>\\t<tagname>\"` (e.g., `\"0001\\tperson\"`)\n- Serial: 8-digit number with 3 trailing zeros (e.g., `\"0001000\"` → display as `\"0001\"`)\n- Purpose: Maintains insertion order (Frontier tables are sorted by name)\n- Function: `serialstring()` (lines 1253-1275) generates next serial number\n\n**Special Table Entries:**\n- `\"/atts\"` - Attribute sub-table (attributes of parent element)\n- `\"/pcdata\"` - Parsed character data (text content)\n- `\"/cdata\"` - CDATA section content\n- `\"/contents\"` - Element text when element has attributes\n- `\"/comment\"` - XML comment content\n- `\"/doctype\"` - DOCTYPE declaration\n- `\"/pi\"` - Processing instruction\n\n**Empty vs. Non-Empty Tags:**\n- Empty tag `<tag/>` with no attributes → stored as empty string (`\"\"`)\n- Empty tag with attributes → stored as table with `\"/pcdata\" = \"\"`\n- Non-empty tag always stored as table (even if no attributes)\n\n### Helper Functions (Already Implemented)\n\n**Name Manipulation:**\n```c\n// langxml.c line 2238 - Extract display name from serialized name\nboolean xmlgetname(bigstring bsname);\n// Input:  \"0001\\tperson\"\n// Output: \"person\" (also encodes XML entities)\n\n// langxml.c line 2938 - Check if hash node name matches display name\nboolean isxmlmatch(hdlhashnode hn, bigstring name);\n// Returns true if hn's name (after tab) equals 'name'\n```\n\n**Table Navigation:**\n```c\n// langxml.c line 2441 - Get table value from hash node\nboolean gethashnodetable(hdlhashnode hn, hdlhashtable *ht);\n// Converts external table value to table handle\n\n// langxml.c line 3033 - Get /atts sub-table\nboolean xmlgetattribute(hdlhashtable ht, bigstring name, hdlhashtable *adratts);\n// Returns attribute table from element table\n```\n\n**Table Creation:**\n```c\n// langxml.c line 1278 - Generate new item address with serial prefix\nstatic void getnewitemaddress(hdlhashtable ht, bigstring bs, xmladdress *adr);\n// Creates address like \"0002\\tnewtag\" in table ht\n\n// langxml.c line 1253 - Generate next serial number\nstatic boolean serialstring(hdlhashtable ht, bigstring serializedname);\n// Returns \"0001000\\t\" (next available serial)\n```\n\n---\n\n## Verb Implementation Matrix\n\n### Complexity Categories\n\n**Tier 1 - Simple (2-4 hours each):**\n- Direct mapping to existing C functions\n- Minimal new logic required\n- Straightforward parameter handling\n\n**Tier 2 - Moderate (4-8 hours each):**\n- Requires new helper functions\n- Multiple table operations\n- Some state management\n\n**Tier 3 - Complex (8-16 hours each):**\n- Significant new functionality\n- Deep parser integration\n- Multiple edge cases\n\n---\n\n## Phase 1: Core Parser Interface (Tier 3)\n\n**Duration:** 2-3 days\n**Prerequisites:** None (foundational verbs)\n\n### 1.1 xml.compile(s, adrTable)\n\n**Complexity:** Tier 3 (High - Already 95% implemented)\n**Status:** C implementation exists, needs verb wrapper\n**Estimated Effort:** 2-4 hours\n\n**Implementation:**\n\n```c\n// In tests/headless_xml_verbs.c, case xmlv_compile:\nstatic boolean xmlcompileverb(hdltreenode hp1, tyvaluerecord *v) {\n    Handle htext;\n    hdlhashtable ht;\n    bigstring bs;\n    xmladdress xmladr;\n\n    // Get XML string parameter\n    if (!getstringvalue(hp1, 1, &htext))\n        return false;\n\n    // Get destination table address\n    flnextparamislast = true;\n    if (!getaddressparam(hp1, 2, v))\n        return false;\n\n    if (!getaddressvalue(*v, &ht, bs))\n        return false;\n\n    // Prepare xmladdress structure\n    xmladr.ht = ht;\n    copystring(bs, xmladr.bs);\n\n    // Call existing parser (langxml.c line 1834)\n    if (!xmlcompile(htext, &xmladr))\n        return false;\n\n    return setbooleanvalue(true, v);\n}\n```\n\n**Existing C Function:** `xmlcompile()` (langxml.c lines 1834-2235)\n\n**What It Does:**\n- Tokenizes XML text using `getnexttoken()`\n- Builds nested table structure with serialized names\n- Handles tags, attributes, CDATA, comments, processing instructions\n- Validates XML structure (matching open/close tags)\n- Populates destination table at `adrTable`\n\n**Known Edge Cases (Already Handled):**\n- Namespaces (stored in `/atts` with key \"namespace\")\n- Self-closing tags (`<tag/>`)\n- Empty tags with attributes\n- CDATA sections\n- XML comments (stored under \"/comment\" key if `#ifdef compileall`)\n- DOCTYPE declarations\n- Processing instructions (`<?xml ...?>`)\n\n**Test Cases Needed:**\n```yaml\n- name: \"xml.compile - simple element\"\n  script: |\n    local(t);\n    new(tableType, @t);\n    xml.compile(\"<person>John</person>\", @t);\n    return t.[\"0001\\tperson\"]\n  expected_output: \"John\"\n\n- name: \"xml.compile - nested elements\"\n  script: |\n    local(t);\n    new(tableType, @t);\n    xml.compile(\"<root><child>value</child></root>\", @t);\n    return t.[\"0001\\troot\"].[\"0001\\tchild\"]\n  expected_output: \"value\"\n\n- name: \"xml.compile - element with attributes\"\n  script: |\n    local(t);\n    new(tableType, @t);\n    xml.compile(\"<person name='John' age='30'/>\", @t);\n    return t.[\"0001\\tperson\"].[\"/atts\"].name\n  expected_output: \"John\"\n\n- name: \"xml.compile - CDATA section\"\n  script: |\n    local(t);\n    new(tableType, @t);\n    xml.compile(\"<code><![CDATA[if (x < 5)]]></code>\", @t);\n    return t.[\"0001\\tcode\"].[\"/cdata\"]\n  expected_output: \"if (x < 5)\"\n```\n\n**Error Conditions:**\n- Invalid XML structure → `scriptError(badxmltexterror, ...)`\n- Mismatched tags → Error with offset\n- Truncated XML → Error message\n\n---\n\n### 1.2 xml.decompile(adrTable)\n\n**Complexity:** Tier 3 (High - Already 95% implemented)\n**Status:** C implementation exists, needs verb wrapper\n**Estimated Effort:** 2-4 hours\n\n**Implementation:**\n\n```c\n// In tests/headless_xml_verbs.c, case xmlv_decompile:\nstatic boolean xmldecompileverb(hdltreenode hp1, tyvaluerecord *v) {\n    hdlhashtable ht;\n    bigstring bs;\n    Handle htext = nil;\n\n    // Get source table address\n    flnextparamislast = true;\n    if (!getaddressparam(hp1, 1, v))\n        return false;\n\n    if (!getaddressvalue(*v, &ht, bs))\n        return false;\n\n    // Look up the table value\n    hdlhashnode hnode;\n    if (!hashtablelookupnode(ht, bs, &hnode))\n        return false;\n\n    // Get the table handle\n    tyvaluerecord tableval = (**hnode).val;\n    if (!langexternalvaltotable(tableval, &ht, hnode))\n        return false;\n\n    // Call existing decompiler (langxml.c line 2869)\n    if (!xmldecompile(ht, &htext))\n        return false;\n\n    return setheapvalue(htext, stringvaluetype, v);\n}\n```\n\n**Existing C Function:** `xmldecompile()` (langxml.c lines 2869-2935)\n\n**What It Does:**\n- Traverses table structure recursively\n- Generates XML tags with proper nesting\n- Handles attributes (from `/atts` sub-table)\n- Encodes special characters (`&`, `<`, `>`, `\"`)\n- Maintains indentation for pretty-printing\n- Returns XML text as string\n\n**Algorithm (from existing code):**\n1. Check if first item is `?xml` declaration (if not, add default)\n2. Traverse sorted hash nodes (maintains order via serial prefixes)\n3. For each node:\n   - If table → open tag, recurse, close tag\n   - If string → emit text content\n   - If attributes exist → emit as `name=\"value\"` pairs\n4. Handle special keys (`/pcdata`, `/cdata`, `/atts`, `/pi`, `/comment`)\n\n**Test Cases Needed:**\n```yaml\n- name: \"xml.decompile - simple element\"\n  script: |\n    local(t);\n    new(tableType, @t);\n    t.[\"0001\\tperson\"] = \"John\";\n    return xml.decompile(@t)\n  expected_contains:\n    - \"<person>John</person>\"\n\n- name: \"xml.decompile - nested elements\"\n  script: |\n    local(t);\n    new(tableType, @t);\n    new(tableType, @t.[\"0001\\troot\"]);\n    t.[\"0001\\troot\"].[\"0001\\tchild\"] = \"value\";\n    return xml.decompile(@t)\n  expected_contains:\n    - \"<root>\"\n    - \"<child>value</child>\"\n    - \"</root>\"\n\n- name: \"xml.decompile - element with attributes\"\n  script: |\n    local(t);\n    new(tableType, @t);\n    new(tableType, @t.[\"0001\\tperson\"]);\n    new(tableType, @t.[\"0001\\tperson\"].[\"/atts\"]);\n    t.[\"0001\\tperson\"].[\"/atts\"].name = \"John\";\n    t.[\"0001\\tperson\"].[\"/atts\"].age = \"30\";\n    t.[\"0001\\tperson\"].[\"/pcdata\"] = \"\";\n    return xml.decompile(@t)\n  expected_pattern: \"<person\\\\s+name=\\\"John\\\"\\\\s+age=\\\"30\\\"\\\\s*/>\"\n```\n\n**Error Conditions:**\n- Empty table → `scriptError(cantdecompileemptytableerror, ...)`\n- Invalid table structure → Error message\n\n---\n\n## Phase 2: Tree Navigation (Tier 1-2)\n\n**Duration:** 1-2 days\n**Prerequisites:** Phase 1 (compile/decompile)\n\n### 2.1 xml.getAddress(adrTable, name)\n\n**Complexity:** Tier 1 (Simple)\n**Status:** Partially implemented (helper exists)\n**Estimated Effort:** 2 hours\n\n**Implementation:**\n\n```c\n// In tests/headless_xml_verbs.c, case xmlv_getaddress:\nstatic boolean xmlgetaddressverb(hdltreenode hp1, tyvaluerecord *v) {\n    hdlhashtable ht;\n    bigstring bstable, name;\n    tyvaluerecord vtable;\n\n    // Get table address\n    if (!getaddressparam(hp1, 1, &vtable))\n        return false;\n\n    if (!getaddressvalue(vtable, &ht, bstable))\n        return false;\n\n    // Get name parameter\n    flnextparamislast = true;\n    if (!getstringvalue(hp1, 2, name))\n        return false;\n\n    // Call helper function (langxml.c line 2948)\n    if (!xmlgetaddress(ht, name))  // Modifies 'name' to include serial prefix\n        return false;\n\n    // Return address of found item\n    return setaddressvalue(ht, name, v);\n}\n```\n\n**Existing Helper:** `xmlgetaddress()` (langxml.c lines 2948-2979)\n\n**What It Does:**\n- Searches table for first item matching display name (after `\\t`)\n- Uses `isxmlmatch()` to compare names\n- Modifies `name` parameter to include serial prefix (e.g., `\"person\"` → `\"0001\\tperson\"`)\n- Returns false if not found\n\n**Test Cases:**\n```yaml\n- name: \"xml.getAddress - find first element\"\n  script: |\n    local(t);\n    new(tableType, @t);\n    t.[\"0001\\tperson\"] = \"John\";\n    t.[\"0002\\tperson\"] = \"Jane\";\n    local(adr = xml.getAddress(@t, \"person\"));\n    return adr^\n  expected_output: \"John\"\n\n- name: \"xml.getAddress - element not found\"\n  script: |\n    local(t);\n    new(tableType, @t);\n    t.[\"0001\\tperson\"] = \"John\";\n    try { xml.getAddress(@t, \"missing\"); return \"no error\" }\n    else { return \"error\" }\n  expected_output: \"error\"\n```\n\n---\n\n### 2.2 xml.getAddressList(adrTable, name)\n\n**Complexity:** Tier 2 (Moderate)\n**Status:** Partially implemented (helper exists)\n**Estimated Effort:** 3-4 hours\n\n**Implementation:**\n\n```c\n// In tests/headless_xml_verbs.c, case xmlv_getaddresslist:\nstatic boolean xmlgetaddresslistverb(hdltreenode hp1, tyvaluerecord *v) {\n    hdlhashtable ht;\n    bigstring bstable, name;\n    tyvaluerecord vtable;\n\n    // Get table address\n    if (!getaddressparam(hp1, 1, &vtable))\n        return false;\n\n    if (!getaddressvalue(vtable, &ht, bstable))\n        return false;\n\n    // Get name parameter\n    flnextparamislast = true;\n    if (!getstringvalue(hp1, 2, name))\n        return false;\n\n    // Call helper function (langxml.c line 2982)\n    // Note: This helper returns a list table, not a list value\n    // Need to convert to proper Frontier list\n\n    hdlhashtable htlist;\n    hdllistrecord hlist;\n\n    if (!xmlgetaddresslist(ht, name, &htlist))\n        return false;\n\n    // Convert hash table to list value\n    if (!langhashtabletolist(htlist, &hlist))\n        return false;\n\n    return setlistvalue(hlist, v);\n}\n```\n\n**Existing Helper:** `xmlgetaddresslist()` (langxml.c lines 2982-3030)\n\n**What It Does:**\n- Searches table for ALL items matching display name\n- Creates new table with serialized names for each match\n- Returns addresses as table entries (need to convert to list)\n\n**Note:** The existing helper returns a table, but the verb should return a list. Need to add conversion logic.\n\n**Test Cases:**\n```yaml\n- name: \"xml.getAddressList - multiple matches\"\n  script: |\n    local(t);\n    new(tableType, @t);\n    t.[\"0001\\tperson\"] = \"John\";\n    t.[\"0002\\tperson\"] = \"Jane\";\n    t.[\"0003\\tperson\"] = \"Bob\";\n    local(lst = xml.getAddressList(@t, \"person\"));\n    return sizeOf(lst)\n  expected_output: \"3\"\n\n- name: \"xml.getAddressList - no matches\"\n  script: |\n    local(t);\n    new(tableType, @t);\n    local(lst = xml.getAddressList(@t, \"missing\"));\n    return sizeOf(lst)\n  expected_output: \"0\"\n```\n\n---\n\n### 2.3 xml.getAttribute(adrTable, name)\n\n**Complexity:** Tier 1 (Simple)\n**Status:** Already implemented in C\n**Estimated Effort:** 1 hour (wrapper only)\n\n**Implementation:**\n\n```c\n// In tests/headless_xml_verbs.c, case xmlv_getattribute:\nstatic boolean xmlgetattributeverb(hdltreenode hp1, tyvaluerecord *v) {\n    hdlhashtable ht;\n    bigstring bstable, name;\n    tyvaluerecord vtable;\n\n    // Get table address\n    if (!getaddressparam(hp1, 1, &vtable))\n        return false;\n\n    if (!getaddressvalue(vtable, &ht, bstable))\n        return false;\n\n    // Resolve table value\n    hdlhashnode hnode;\n    if (!hashtablelookupnode(ht, bstable, &hnode))\n        return false;\n\n    tyvaluerecord elval = (**hnode).val;\n    if (!langexternalvaltotable(elval, &ht, hnode))\n        return false;\n\n    // Get attribute name\n    flnextparamislast = true;\n    if (!getstringvalue(hp1, 2, name))\n        return false;\n\n    // Call helper (langxml.c line 3033)\n    hdlhashtable attstable;\n    if (!xmlgetattribute(ht, name, &attstable))\n        return false;\n\n    // Return address of attribute\n    return setaddressvalue(attstable, name, v);\n}\n```\n\n**Existing Implementation:** `xmlgetattribute()` (langxml.c lines 3033-3064)\n**Existing Verb Implementation:** `xmlgetattributeverb()` (langxml.c lines 3404-3432)\n\n**What It Does:**\n- Looks up `/atts` sub-table in element table\n- Returns address of attribute value in `/atts` table\n\n**Test Cases:**\n```yaml\n- name: \"xml.getAttribute - existing attribute\"\n  script: |\n    local(t);\n    xml.compile(\"<person name='John' age='30'/>\", @t);\n    local(adr = xml.getAttribute(@t.[\"0001\\tperson\"], \"name\"));\n    return adr^\n  expected_output: \"John\"\n```\n\n---\n\n### 2.4 xml.getAttributeValue(adrTable, name)\n\n**Complexity:** Tier 1 (Simple)\n**Status:** Already implemented in C\n**Estimated Effort:** 1 hour (wrapper only)\n\n**Implementation:**\n\n```c\n// Similar to getAttribute but returns value instead of address\nstatic boolean xmlgetattributevalueverb(hdltreenode hp1, tyvaluerecord *v) {\n    // ... same setup as getAttribute ...\n\n    hdlhashtable attstable;\n    if (!xmlgetattribute(ht, name, &attstable))\n        return false;\n\n    // Look up value in attribute table\n    hdlhashnode hnode;\n    tyvaluerecord val;\n    if (!hashtablelookup(attstable, name, &val, &hnode))\n        return false;\n\n    // Return copy of value\n    return copyvaluerecord(val, v);\n}\n```\n\n**Existing Implementation:** `xmlgetattributevalueverb()` (langxml.c lines 3435-3461)\n\n**Test Cases:**\n```yaml\n- name: \"xml.getAttributeValue - existing attribute\"\n  script: |\n    local(t);\n    xml.compile(\"<person name='John' age='30'/>\", @t);\n    return xml.getAttributeValue(@t.[\"0001\\tperson\"], \"age\")\n  expected_output: \"30\"\n```\n\n---\n\n### 2.5 xml.getPathAddress(adrTable, path, adrResult)\n\n**Complexity:** Tier 2 (Moderate)\n**Status:** Partially implemented (UserTalk version exists, C helper exists)\n**Estimated Effort:** 4 hours\n\n**Implementation:**\n\nThe UserTalk version exists in `xml.getPathAddress` but there's also a C helper `xmlgetpathaddress()` at line 3067. Need to expose as kernel verb.\n\n```c\n// In tests/headless_xml_verbs.c, case xmlv_getpathaddress:\nstatic boolean xmlgetpathaddressverb(hdltreenode hp1, tyvaluerecord *v) {\n    hdlhashtable ht;\n    bigstring bstable;\n    Handle hpath;\n    tyvaluerecord vtable, vresult;\n    boolean flvalid;\n\n    // Get source table address\n    if (!getaddressparam(hp1, 1, &vtable))\n        return false;\n\n    if (!getaddressvalue(vtable, &ht, bstable))\n        return false;\n\n    // Get path string\n    if (!getstringvalue(hp1, 2, &hpath))\n        return false;\n\n    // Get result address parameter\n    flnextparamislast = true;\n    if (!getaddressparam(hp1, 3, &vresult))\n        return false;\n\n    // Prepare source address\n    tyaddress addrsource;\n    addrsource.ht = ht;\n    copystring(bstable, addrsource.bs);\n\n    // Prepare result address\n    tyaddress adrresult;\n    if (!getaddressvalue(vresult, &adrresult.ht, adrresult.bs))\n        return false;\n\n    // Call helper (langxml.c line 3067)\n    if (!xmlgetpathaddress(&addrsource, hpath, &adrresult, &flvalid))\n        return false;\n\n    if (!flvalid)\n        return false;  // Path not found\n\n    // Assign result address\n    tyvaluerecord vaddr;\n    if (!setaddressvalue(adrresult.ht, adrresult.bs, &vaddr))\n        return false;\n\n    return langassignfunctionresult(&vresult, &vaddr);\n}\n```\n\n**Existing Helper:** `xmlgetpathaddress()` (langxml.c lines 3067-3120)\n\n**What It Does:**\n- Navigates path like `\"person/address/city\"`\n- Splits path by `/` delimiter\n- Traverses table hierarchy\n- Assigns result to `adrResult` parameter\n\n**Test Cases:**\n```yaml\n- name: \"xml.getPathAddress - simple path\"\n  script: |\n    local(t, result);\n    xml.compile(\"<root><child>value</child></root>\", @t);\n    xml.getPathAddress(@t, \"root/child\", @result);\n    return result\n  expected_output: \"value\"\n\n- name: \"xml.getPathAddress - invalid path\"\n  script: |\n    local(t, result);\n    xml.compile(\"<root><child>value</child></root>\", @t);\n    try { xml.getPathAddress(@t, \"root/missing\", @result); return \"no error\" }\n    else { return \"error\" }\n  expected_output: \"error\"\n```\n\n---\n\n## Phase 3: Tree Construction (Tier 2)\n\n**Duration:** 1-2 days\n**Prerequisites:** Phase 2 (navigation verbs)\n\n### 3.1 xml.addTable(adrParent, name)\n\n**Complexity:** Tier 2 (Moderate)\n**Status:** Needs implementation\n**Estimated Effort:** 4 hours\n\n**Implementation:**\n\n```c\n// In tests/headless_xml_verbs.c, case xmlv_addtable:\nstatic boolean xmladdtableverb(hdltreenode hp1, tyvaluerecord *v) {\n    hdlhashtable htparent;\n    bigstring bsparent, name;\n    tyvaluerecord vparent;\n    xmladdress adrnewitem;\n    hdlhashtable htnew;\n\n    // Get parent table address\n    if (!getaddressparam(hp1, 1, &vparent))\n        return false;\n\n    if (!getaddressvalue(vparent, &htparent, bsparent))\n        return false;\n\n    // Resolve parent to table\n    hdlhashnode hnode;\n    if (!hashtablelookupnode(htparent, bsparent, &hnode))\n        return false;\n\n    tyvaluerecord parentval = (**hnode).val;\n    if (!langexternalvaltotable(parentval, &htparent, hnode))\n        return false;\n\n    // Get name parameter\n    flnextparamislast = true;\n    if (!getstringvalue(hp1, 2, name))\n        return false;\n\n    // Generate serialized name and create new table\n    // Use helper from langxml.c line 1278\n    getnewitemaddress(htparent, name, &adrnewitem);\n\n    // Create new table at address\n    if (!langassignnewtablevalue(adrnewitem.ht, adrnewitem.bs, &htnew))\n        return false;\n\n    // Return address of new table\n    return setaddressvalue(adrnewitem.ht, adrnewitem.bs, v);\n}\n```\n\n**New Helper Needed:**\n\n```c\n// In Common/source/langxml.c:\n// Expose getnewitemaddress() as non-static, or add wrapper\n```\n\n**What It Does:**\n- Creates new sub-table in parent with serialized name\n- Returns address of newly created table\n\n**Test Cases:**\n```yaml\n- name: \"xml.addTable - create sub-table\"\n  script: |\n    local(t);\n    new(tableType, @t);\n    local(adr = xml.addTable(@t, \"person\"));\n    return nameOf(adr^)\n  expected_pattern: \"^0001\\\\tperson$\"\n\n- name: \"xml.addTable - multiple tables maintain order\"\n  script: |\n    local(t);\n    new(tableType, @t);\n    xml.addTable(@t, \"first\");\n    xml.addTable(@t, \"second\");\n    return nameOf(parentOf(t.[\"0002\\tsecond\"]^).[\"0001\\tfirst\"]^)\n  expected_output: \"0001\\tfirst\"\n```\n\n---\n\n### 3.2 xml.addValue(adrParent, name, value)\n\n**Complexity:** Tier 2 (Moderate)\n**Status:** Needs implementation\n**Estimated Effort:** 3 hours\n\n**Implementation:**\n\n```c\n// In tests/headless_xml_verbs.c, case xmlv_addvalue:\nstatic boolean xmladdvalueverb(hdltreenode hp1, tyvaluerecord *v) {\n    hdlhashtable htparent;\n    bigstring bsparent, name;\n    tyvaluerecord vparent, vvalue;\n    xmladdress adrnewitem;\n\n    // Get parent table address\n    if (!getaddressparam(hp1, 1, &vparent))\n        return false;\n\n    if (!getaddressvalue(vparent, &htparent, bsparent))\n        return false;\n\n    // Resolve parent to table\n    hdlhashnode hnode;\n    if (!hashtablelookupnode(htparent, bsparent, &hnode))\n        return false;\n\n    tyvaluerecord parentval = (**hnode).val;\n    if (!langexternalvaltotable(parentval, &htparent, hnode))\n        return false;\n\n    // Get name parameter\n    if (!getstringvalue(hp1, 2, name))\n        return false;\n\n    // Get value parameter\n    flnextparamislast = true;\n    if (!getparamvalue(hp1, 3, &vvalue))\n        return false;\n\n    // Generate serialized name\n    getnewitemaddress(htparent, name, &adrnewitem);\n\n    // Assign value to new item\n    if (!hashtableassign(adrnewitem.ht, adrnewitem.bs, vvalue))\n        return false;\n\n    // Return address of new value\n    return setaddressvalue(adrnewitem.ht, adrnewitem.bs, v);\n}\n```\n\n**Test Cases:**\n```yaml\n- name: \"xml.addValue - create string value\"\n  script: |\n    local(t);\n    new(tableType, @t);\n    local(adr = xml.addValue(@t, \"name\", \"John\"));\n    return adr^\n  expected_output: \"John\"\n\n- name: \"xml.addValue - create numeric value\"\n  script: |\n    local(t);\n    new(tableType, @t);\n    xml.addValue(@t, \"age\", 30);\n    return t.[\"0001\\tage\"]\n  expected_output: \"30\"\n```\n\n---\n\n## Phase 4: Value Serialization (Tier 2-3)\n\n**Duration:** 2-3 days\n**Prerequisites:** Phase 1 (compile/decompile)\n\n### 4.1 xml.valToString(val, indentLevel)\n\n**Complexity:** Tier 2 (Moderate)\n**Status:** Partial implementation exists\n**Estimated Effort:** 4-6 hours\n\n**Implementation:**\n\n```c\n// In tests/headless_xml_verbs.c, case xmlv_valtostring:\nstatic boolean xmlvaltostringverb(hdltreenode hp1, tyvaluerecord *v) {\n    tyvaluerecord val;\n    long indentlevel = 0;\n    Handle hstring;\n\n    // Get value parameter\n    if (!getparamvalue(hp1, 1, &val))\n        return false;\n\n    // Get indent level (optional, defaults to 0)\n    flnextparamislast = true;\n    if (!getlongvalue(hp1, 2, &indentlevel))\n        indentlevel = 0;  // Use default if not provided\n\n    // Call helper (langxml.c line 2262)\n    // Note: xmlvaltostring has different signature than xmlfrontiervaltotaggedtext\n    if (!xmlvaltostring(val, (short)indentlevel, true, &hstring))\n        return false;\n\n    return setheapvalue(hstring, stringvaluetype, v);\n}\n```\n\n**Existing Helper:** `xmlvaltostring()` (langxml.c lines 2262-2437)\n\n**What It Does:**\n- Converts scalar Frontier values to XML-RPC tagged text\n- Similar to `xmlfrontiervaltotaggedtext()` but different format\n- Handles: strings, numbers, booleans, dates, binary data\n\n**Difference from frontiervaluetotaggedtext:**\n- `valToString`: Converts ANY value type (scalars, tables, lists)\n- `frontiervaluetotaggedtext`: Specifically for XML-RPC protocol format (includes `<value>` wrappers)\n\n**Note:** There may be redundancy here. The existing `xmlfrontiervaltotaggedtext()` might be sufficient. Need to verify exact use cases.\n\n**Test Cases:**\n```yaml\n- name: \"xml.valToString - string\"\n  script: |\n    return xml.valToString(\"hello\", 0)\n  expected_output: \"<string>hello</string>\"\n\n- name: \"xml.valToString - integer\"\n  script: |\n    return xml.valToString(42, 0)\n  expected_output: \"<i4>42</i4>\"\n```\n\n---\n\n### 4.2 xml.structToFrontierValue(adrStruct, adrFrontierVal)\n\n**Complexity:** Tier 3 (Complex)\n**Status:** Partial implementation exists\n**Estimated Effort:** 8-12 hours\n\n**Implementation:**\n\n```c\n// In tests/headless_xml_verbs.c, case xmlv_structtofrontiervalue:\nstatic boolean xmlstructtofrontiervalueverb(hdltreenode hp1, tyvaluerecord *v) {\n    tyaddress addrstruct, addrresult;\n    tyvaluerecord vstruct, vresult;\n\n    // Get struct address\n    if (!getaddressparam(hp1, 1, &vstruct))\n        return false;\n\n    if (!getaddressvalue(vstruct, &addrstruct.ht, addrstruct.bs))\n        return false;\n\n    // Get result address\n    flnextparamislast = true;\n    if (!getaddressparam(hp1, 2, &vresult))\n        return false;\n\n    if (!getaddressvalue(vresult, &addrresult.ht, addrresult.bs))\n        return false;\n\n    // Call helper (langxml.c line 550)\n    tyvaluerecord vconverted;\n    if (!xmlstructtofrontiervalue(&addrstruct, &vconverted))\n        return false;\n\n    // Assign converted value to result address\n    return langassignfunctionresult(&vresult, &vconverted);\n}\n```\n\n**Existing Helper:** `xmlstructtofrontiervalue()` (langxml.c lines 550-650)\n\n**What It Does:**\n- Converts XML-RPC struct (compiled XML table) to native Frontier value\n- Handles type coercion based on XML tag names\n- Inverse of `xmlfrontiervaltotaggedtext()`\n\n**Algorithm:**\n1. Detect type from table structure:\n   - `<struct>` → table\n   - `<array>` → list\n   - `<i4>`, `<int>` → integer\n   - `<boolean>` → boolean\n   - `<double>` → double\n   - `<string>` → string\n   - `<dateTime.iso8601>` → date\n   - `<base64>` → binary\n2. Recursively convert nested structures\n3. Assign result to destination address\n\n**Test Cases:**\n```yaml\n- name: \"xml.structToFrontierValue - integer\"\n  script: |\n    local(t, result);\n    xml.compile(\"<i4>42</i4>\", @t);\n    xml.structToFrontierValue(@t.[\"0001\\ti4\"], @result);\n    return result\n  expected_output: \"42\"\n\n- name: \"xml.structToFrontierValue - struct\"\n  script: |\n    local(t, result);\n    xml.compile(\"<struct><member><name>x</name><value><i4>10</i4></value></member></struct>\", @t);\n    xml.structToFrontierValue(@t.[\"0001\\tstruct\"], @result);\n    return result.x\n  expected_output: \"10\"\n\n- name: \"xml.structToFrontierValue - array\"\n  script: |\n    local(t, result);\n    xml.compile(\"<array><data><value><i4>1</i4></value><value><i4>2</i4></value></data></array>\", @t);\n    xml.structToFrontierValue(@t.[\"0001\\tarray\"], @result);\n    return sizeOf(result)\n  expected_output: \"2\"\n```\n\n**Implementation Challenges:**\n- Type detection based on tag names\n- Handling nested structures correctly\n- Error handling for malformed XML-RPC structures\n\n---\n\n## Phase 5: Utility Verbs (Tier 1)\n\n**Duration:** 0.5 day\n**Prerequisites:** Phase 2 (navigation)\n\n### 5.1 xml.convertToDisplayName(name)\n\n**Complexity:** Tier 1 (Simple)\n**Status:** Helper exists\n**Estimated Effort:** 1 hour\n\n**Implementation:**\n\n```c\n// In tests/headless_xml_verbs.c, case xmlv_converttodisplayname:\nstatic boolean xmlconverttodisplaynameverb(hdltreenode hp1, tyvaluerecord *v) {\n    bigstring name;\n\n    // Get name parameter\n    flnextparamislast = true;\n    if (!getstringvalue(hp1, 1, name))\n        return false;\n\n    // Strip serial prefix (extract part after \\t)\n    bigstring displayname;\n    if (!nthword(name, 2, '\\t', displayname)) {\n        // No tab found, name is already display name\n        copystring(name, displayname);\n    }\n\n    return setstringvalue(displayname, v);\n}\n```\n\n**What It Does:**\n- Strips serial prefix from XML item names\n- Input: `\"0001\\tperson\"` → Output: `\"person\"`\n\n**Test Cases:**\n```yaml\n- name: \"xml.convertToDisplayName - with prefix\"\n  script: |\n    return xml.convertToDisplayName(\"0001\\tperson\")\n  expected_output: \"person\"\n\n- name: \"xml.convertToDisplayName - no prefix\"\n  script: |\n    return xml.convertToDisplayName(\"person\")\n  expected_output: \"person\"\n```\n\n---\n\n## Implementation Schedule\n\n### Week 1: Foundation (8-10 hours)\n**Days 1-2:**\n- ✅ Set up feature branch: `feature/xml-verbs`\n- ✅ Update `tests/headless_xml_verbs.c` header comments\n- ✅ Implement Phase 1.1: `xml.compile()` verb wrapper\n- ✅ Implement Phase 1.2: `xml.decompile()` verb wrapper\n- ✅ Write integration tests for compile/decompile\n- ✅ Run test suite, fix any issues\n\n### Week 2: Navigation & Construction (12-16 hours)\n**Days 3-4:**\n- ✅ Implement Phase 2: All navigation verbs (getAddress, getAddressList, getAttribute, getAttributeValue, getPathAddress)\n- ✅ Write integration tests for navigation\n- ✅ Implement Phase 3: Construction verbs (addTable, addValue)\n- ✅ Write integration tests for construction\n- ✅ Run test suite\n\n**Days 5-6:**\n- ✅ Implement Phase 4.1: `xml.valToString()`\n- ✅ Implement Phase 5.1: `xml.convertToDisplayName()`\n- ✅ Write integration tests\n- ✅ Run test suite\n\n### Week 3: Complex Conversions & Polish (12-16 hours)\n**Days 7-8:**\n- ✅ Implement Phase 4.2: `xml.structToFrontierValue()` (complex)\n- ✅ Write comprehensive integration tests\n- ✅ Test round-trip conversions (compile → structToFrontierValue → frontiervaluetotaggedtext)\n\n**Days 9-10:**\n- ✅ Code review and refactoring\n- ✅ Documentation updates\n- ✅ Edge case testing\n- ✅ Performance testing (large XML documents)\n- ✅ Create PR\n\n---\n\n## Testing Strategy\n\n### Unit Test Coverage\n\n**For Each Verb:**\n1. Happy path (normal usage)\n2. Edge cases (empty values, special characters)\n3. Error conditions (missing params, invalid types)\n4. Boundary conditions (max depth, max size)\n\n### Integration Test Suites\n\n**Round-Trip Tests:**\n```yaml\n- name: \"XML round-trip - compile → decompile\"\n  script: |\n    local(xml = \"<person><name>John</name><age>30</age></person>\");\n    local(t);\n    new(tableType, @t);\n    xml.compile(xml, @t);\n    local(result = xml.decompile(@t));\n    return result\n  expected_contains:\n    - \"<person>\"\n    - \"<name>John</name>\"\n    - \"<age>30</age>\"\n    - \"</person>\"\n\n- name: \"Value conversion round-trip\"\n  script: |\n    local(t);\n    new(tableType, @t);\n    t.x = 42;\n    t.y = \"hello\";\n    local(xml = xml.frontiervaluetotaggedtext(@t, 0));\n    local(compiled);\n    new(tableType, @compiled);\n    xml.compile(xml, @compiled);\n    local(result);\n    xml.structToFrontierValue(@compiled.[\"0001\\tstruct\"], @result);\n    return result.x\n  expected_output: \"42\"\n```\n\n**OPML Tests (Real-World Format):**\n```yaml\n- name: \"OPML compile and navigate\"\n  script: |\n    local(opml = \"<?xml version='1.0'?><opml><body><outline text='Item 1'/></body></opml>\");\n    local(t);\n    new(tableType, @t);\n    xml.compile(opml, @t);\n    local(body = xml.getAddress(@t, \"opml\"));\n    return nameOf(body^)\n  expected_pattern: \"^0001\\\\topml$\"\n```\n\n**Performance Tests:**\n```yaml\n- name: \"Large XML document (1000 elements)\"\n  script: |\n    local(xml = \"<root>\");\n    local(i);\n    for i = 1 to 1000 {\n      xml = xml + \"<item>\" + i + \"</item>\"\n    };\n    xml = xml + \"</root>\";\n    local(t);\n    new(tableType, @t);\n    xml.compile(xml, @t);\n    return sizeOf(t.[\"0001\\troot\"]^)\n  expected_output: \"1000\"\n```\n\n---\n\n## Risk Assessment & Mitigation\n\n### High-Risk Areas\n\n**1. Name Encoding/Decoding (Medium Risk)**\n- **Risk:** Serial prefix handling is fragile\n- **Impact:** Incorrect ordering, lookup failures\n- **Mitigation:**\n  - Comprehensive tests for `xmlgetname()`, `isxmlmatch()`\n  - Test with various serial number patterns\n  - Validate against legacy Frontier behavior\n\n**2. Memory Management (High Risk)**\n- **Risk:** Handle leaks in error paths\n- **Impact:** Memory corruption, crashes\n- **Mitigation:**\n  - Careful review of all `disposehandle()` calls\n  - Use guards (`goto exit` pattern) consistently\n  - Test error paths explicitly\n\n**3. Address vs. Value Parameters (Medium Risk)**\n- **Risk:** Confusion between address parameters and value parameters\n- **Impact:** Incorrect assignments, crashes\n- **Mitigation:**\n  - Clear naming conventions in code\n  - Test both input and output address parameters\n  - Document parameter semantics\n\n**4. Recursive Structures (Medium Risk)**\n- **Risk:** Deep nesting causes stack overflow or performance issues\n- **Impact:** Crashes, slow performance\n- **Mitigation:**\n  - Test with deeply nested structures (10+ levels)\n  - Consider iterative alternatives if needed\n  - Document depth limits\n\n**5. Thread Safety (Medium Risk)**\n- **Risk:** Global state issues (see CLAUDE.md warnings)\n- **Impact:** Concurrency bugs in multi-user scenarios\n- **Mitigation:**\n  - Audit for global mutable state\n  - Use thread-local storage where needed\n  - Test under concurrent load (future work)\n\n---\n\n## Documentation Requirements\n\n### Code Comments\n\n**For Each Verb:**\n```c\n/* Verb: xml.compile(s, adrTable)\n *\n * Compiles XML text into hierarchical database structure.\n *\n * Parameters:\n *   s          - String containing XML text\n *   adrTable   - Address of table to populate (modified)\n *\n * Returns:\n *   Boolean - true on success, scriptError on failure\n *\n * Database Structure:\n *   Elements stored with serialized names: \"0001\\ttagname\"\n *   Attributes in \"/atts\" sub-table\n *   Text content in \"/pcdata\" or as direct string\n *\n * Example:\n *   xml.compile(\"<person><name>John</name></person>\", @t)\n *   // t.[\"0001\\tperson\"].[\"0001\\tname\"] = \"John\"\n */\n```\n\n### User-Facing Documentation\n\n**Updates Needed:**\n- Update `docs/usertalk/docserver/xml/*.txt` with headless caveats\n- Add examples to CLI Usage Guide\n- Document differences from GUI Frontier (if any)\n\n### Implementation Notes Document\n\nCreate `planning/phase3/XML_VERBS_IMPLEMENTATION_NOTES.md`:\n- Lessons learned during implementation\n- Non-obvious design decisions\n- Known limitations\n- Future optimization opportunities\n\n---\n\n## Dependencies & Prerequisites\n\n### External Dependencies\n\n**None.** All functionality is self-contained:\n- Custom XML parser (langxml.c)\n- Database table structures (tablestructure.c)\n- Handle management (memory.c)\n\n### Internal Dependencies\n\n**Required Headers:**\n```c\n#include \"frontier.h\"\n#include \"standard.h\"\n#include \"memory.h\"\n#include \"strings.h\"\n#include \"lang.h\"\n#include \"langinternal.h\"\n#include \"tablestructure.h\"\n#include \"langxml.h\"\n```\n\n**Modified Files:**\n1. `tests/headless_xml_verbs.c` - Main implementation\n2. `Common/headers/langxml.h` - May need to expose helpers\n3. `Common/source/langxml.c` - May need to make helpers non-static\n4. `tests/integration/test_cases/xml_verbs.yaml` - Test cases\n\n---\n\n## Success Criteria\n\n### Functional Requirements\n\n**Must Have:**\n- ✅ All 13 verbs implemented and passing tests\n- ✅ Round-trip conversions work correctly\n- ✅ OPML import/export works (via opxml.c integration)\n- ✅ No memory leaks in normal or error paths\n- ✅ Error messages are helpful and accurate\n\n**Should Have:**\n- ✅ Performance comparable to GUI Frontier\n- ✅ Handles deeply nested structures (10+ levels)\n- ✅ Handles large documents (1000+ elements)\n- ✅ Comprehensive test coverage (80%+ line coverage)\n\n**Nice to Have:**\n- ✅ Performance optimizations for common cases\n- ✅ Benchmarks for regression testing\n- ✅ Fuzzing tests for parser robustness\n\n### Quality Gates\n\n**Before PR Submission:**\n1. ✅ All integration tests pass (`cd tests && make test-integration`)\n2. ✅ All unit tests pass (`./tools/run_headless_tests.sh`)\n3. ✅ No compiler warnings\n4. ✅ No memory leaks (valgrind clean)\n5. ✅ Code review by system-architect agent\n6. ✅ Documentation updated\n\n**PR Review Checklist:**\n- [ ] All verb implementations follow established patterns\n- [ ] Error handling is consistent and complete\n- [ ] Memory management is correct (no leaks)\n- [ ] Test coverage is comprehensive\n- [ ] Code is well-commented\n- [ ] No breaking changes to existing behavior\n- [ ] Performance is acceptable\n\n---\n\n## Open Questions & Future Work\n\n### Implementation Questions\n\n**Q1: Should `xmlgetaddresslist()` return a list or a table?**\n- **Current:** Helper returns table\n- **DocServer:** Verb should return list\n- **Resolution:** Convert table to list in verb wrapper\n\n**Q2: Is `xml.valToString()` redundant with `xml.frontiervaluetotaggedtext()`?**\n- **Investigation needed:** Compare use cases in legacy code\n- **Resolution:** May be able to consolidate or alias\n\n**Q3: Should we expose `getnewitemaddress()` as public API?**\n- **Current:** Static function in langxml.c\n- **Need:** Used by `addTable()` and `addValue()`\n- **Resolution:** Either make non-static or add wrapper\n\n**Q4: Thread-safety concerns?**\n- **Risk:** Global state in XML parser\n- **Investigation:** Audit for global mutable state\n- **Resolution:** May need thread-local storage (see ADR-005)\n\n### Future Enhancements\n\n**Performance Optimizations:**\n- Cache serialized name lookups\n- Optimize frequent navigation paths\n- Consider lazy evaluation for large documents\n\n**Feature Additions:**\n- XML validation against DTD/schema\n- XPath-style queries\n- XML namespace support improvements\n- Streaming XML parser for very large documents\n\n**Developer Experience:**\n- Better error messages with line/column numbers\n- Debug mode with verbose logging\n- Profiling tools for performance analysis\n\n---\n\n## Appendix A: File Locations\n\n### Source Files\n\n**Implementation:**\n- `tests/headless_xml_verbs.c` - Verb dispatch and wrappers\n- `Common/source/langxml.c` - Core XML parser and helpers (35492 tokens, lines 1-3650)\n- `Common/headers/langxml.h` - Public XML API\n\n**Integration:**\n- `Common/source/opxml.c` - OPML-specific XML operations\n- `Common/headers/opxml.h` - OPML API\n\n### Test Files\n\n- `tests/integration/test_cases/xml_verbs.yaml` - Integration tests\n- `tests/tmp/integration/xml_*` - Test output files\n\n### Documentation\n\n- `docs/usertalk/docserver/xml/*.txt` - User documentation (DocServer format)\n- `docs/CLI_USAGE_GUIDE.md` - CLI examples\n- `planning/phase3/XML_VERBS_IMPLEMENTATION_PLAN.md` - This document\n\n---\n\n## Appendix B: XML Database Structure Examples\n\n### Simple Element\n\n**XML:**\n```xml\n<person>John</person>\n```\n\n**Database:**\n```\ntable:\n  [\"0001\\tperson\"] = \"John\"\n```\n\n### Element with Attributes\n\n**XML:**\n```xml\n<person name=\"John\" age=\"30\"/>\n```\n\n**Database:**\n```\ntable:\n  [\"0001\\tperson\"] = table:\n    [\"/atts\"] = table:\n      [\"name\"] = \"John\"\n      [\"age\"] = \"30\"\n    [\"/pcdata\"] = \"\"\n```\n\n### Nested Elements\n\n**XML:**\n```xml\n<person>\n  <name>John</name>\n  <age>30</age>\n</person>\n```\n\n**Database:**\n```\ntable:\n  [\"0001\\tperson\"] = table:\n    [\"0001\\tname\"] = \"John\"\n    [\"0002\\tage\"] = \"30\"\n```\n\n### Element with Text and Attributes\n\n**XML:**\n```xml\n<person name=\"John\">Employee</person>\n```\n\n**Database:**\n```\ntable:\n  [\"0001\\tperson\"] = table:\n    [\"/atts\"] = table:\n      [\"name\"] = \"John\"\n    [\"/pcdata\"] = \"Employee\"\n```\n\n### Multiple Elements (Same Name)\n\n**XML:**\n```xml\n<root>\n  <item>A</item>\n  <item>B</item>\n  <item>C</item>\n</root>\n```\n\n**Database:**\n```\ntable:\n  [\"0001\\troot\"] = table:\n    [\"0001\\titem\"] = \"A\"\n    [\"0002\\titem\"] = \"B\"\n    [\"0003\\titem\"] = \"C\"\n```\n\n### CDATA Section\n\n**XML:**\n```xml\n<code><![CDATA[if (x < 5) { }]]></code>\n```\n\n**Database:**\n```\ntable:\n  [\"0001\\tcode\"] = table:\n    [\"/cdata\"] = \"if (x < 5) { }\"\n```\n\n---\n\n## Appendix C: Implementation Checklist\n\n### Phase 1: Core Parser Interface\n- [ ] 1.1 Implement `xml.compile()` verb wrapper\n- [ ] 1.1 Write integration tests for compile\n- [ ] 1.1 Test error handling
