{"parentUuid":"f5bdab19-588e-4a76-b511-125ec085ac49","isSidechain":true,"userType":"external","cwd":"/Users/jake/dev/jsavin/Frontier","sessionId":"fb8cbe5a-3375-43a0-8935-a2a6c9ce6050","version":"2.1.3","gitBranch":"develop","agentId":"acc0d99","slug":"curious-twirling-sifakis","message":{"model":"claude-sonnet-4-5-20250929","id":"msg_01E8MX4E2Chdeb8GVyFZHiyk","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_01SdekHsz83R8Ya2h1Syryrm","name":"Write","input":{"file_path":"/Users/jake/dev/jsavin/Frontier/planning/phase3/XML_VERB_IMPLEMENTATION_PLAN.md","content":"# XML Verb Implementation Plan\n\n**Status**: Ready for Implementation\n**Created**: 2026-01-12\n**Target Branch**: `feature/xml-verbs`\n**Approach**: Test-Driven Development (TDD)\n\n## Executive Summary\n\nFrontier has a custom XML parser (not libxml2) that converts XML strings into a hierarchical database table structure. The parser is already implemented in `Common/source/langxml.c` with 1,834+ lines of existing code. This plan implements the 13 remaining XML kernel verbs by exposing this existing parser infrastructure through the UserTalk API.\n\n**Key Insight**: Most implementation work is **wrapping existing C functions** in the verb dispatcher, not writing new parsers from scratch.\n\n---\n\n## Current Status\n\n### Implemented (2/18 total verbs)\n1. ✅ **xml.frontiervaluetotaggedtext** - C implementation (37 integration tests passing)\n2. ✅ **xml.getPathAddress** - Pure UserTalk implementation (no kernel stub needed)\n\n### Pure UserTalk (4/18 - no kernel implementation needed)\n3. ✅ **xml.entityDecode** - UserTalk only\n4. ✅ **xml.entityEncode** - UserTalk only\n5. ✅ **xml.convertRssFolderToOpml** - UserTalk only\n6. ✅ **xml.rss.flRssItem** - UserTalk only\n\n### To Implement (13/18 remaining kernel verbs)\n- **xml.compile** - Parser (XML string → database table)\n- **xml.decompile** - Serializer (database table → XML string)\n- **xml.valToString** - Scalar → XML-RPC format\n- **xml.structtofrontiervalue** - XML struct → Frontier value\n- **xml.getAddress** - Find first element by name\n- **xml.getAddressList** - Find all elements with same name\n- **xml.getAttribute** - Get attribute by name\n- **xml.getAttributeValue** - Get attribute value\n- **xml.addTable** - Create sub-table element\n- **xml.addValue** - Create value element\n- **xml.convertToDisplayName** - Strip internal encoding from names\n- **xml.frontiervaluetotaggedtext** - (duplicate/related to valToString?)\n\n---\n\n## XML Structure in Frontier's Database\n\n### Serialized Naming Convention\n\nXML elements are stored with **serialized numeric prefixes** to preserve order:\n\n```\n\"0001\\titemName\"    // First item named \"itemName\"\n\"0002\\titemName\"    // Second item with same name\n\"00001000\\tfoo\"     // Serial numbers can have gaps\n```\n\n**Format**: `{8-digit-number}\\t{element-name}`\n\n**Why**: Hash tables don't preserve insertion order, so serial numbers maintain document order.\n\n### Special Sub-Tables\n\n- **`/atts`** - Attributes table (holds XML attributes)\n- **`/contents`** - Text content (for elements with text)\n- **`/pcdata`** - Parsed character data (text nodes)\n- **`/cdata`** - CDATA sections\n- **`/comment`** - XML comments (not usually compiled)\n- **`/doctype`** - DOCTYPE declarations\n- **`/pi`** - Processing instructions\n\n### Example Structure\n\n**XML Input**:\n```xml\n<person age=\"30\">\n  <name>John</name>\n  <city>Boston</city>\n</person>\n```\n\n**Database Table Structure**:\n```\ntable {\n  \"0001\\tperson\" {\n    \"/atts\" {\n      \"age\" = \"30\"\n    }\n    \"0001\\tname\" {\n      \"/contents\" = \"John\"\n    }\n    \"0002\\tcity\" {\n      \"/contents\" = \"Boston\"\n    }\n  }\n}\n```\n\n---\n\n## Existing Parser Infrastructure (Already Implemented)\n\n### Core Parser Functions (langxml.c)\n\n| Function | Line | Purpose |\n|----------|------|---------|\n| `xmlcompile()` | 1834 | **Main parser** - XML string → table structure |\n| `xmldecompile()` | 2869 | **Serializer** - table structure → XML string |\n| `xmlgetaddress()` | 2962 | Find first element by name |\n| `xmlgetaddresslist()` | 2986 | Find all elements with same name |\n| `xmlgetname()` | 2238 | Strip serial prefix from name |\n| `isxmlmatch()` | 2938 | Match element by name (ignoring serial) |\n| `xmlgetattribute()` | (header) | Get attribute table |\n| `xmlfrontiervaltotaggedtext()` | 380 | Frontier value → XML-RPC (already exposed) |\n| `xmlstructtofrontiervalue()` | 703 | XML struct → Frontier value |\n\n### Tokenizer Functions (langxml.c)\n\n- `getnexttoken()` - Lexical analyzer\n- `newxmltoken()` / `disposexmltoken()` - Token lifecycle\n- `assignxmltoken()` - Token assignment\n- `createtokentable()` - Create table from token\n- `serialstring()` - Generate serial numbers\n- `getnewitemaddress()` - Create serialized table entry\n\n### Helper Functions\n\n- `replaceallinhandle()` - Entity encoding/decoding\n- `handlebeginswith()` / `handlecontains()` - String matching\n- `trimtrailingwhitespace()` - Whitespace normalization\n- `breakatfirstwhitespacechar()` - Tag parsing\n\n---\n\n## Implementation Strategy\n\n### Phase 1: Core Parser/Serializer (Highest Priority)\n**Goal**: Enable XML → Table → XML round-trip\n\n**Verbs**:\n1. **xml.compile(s, adrTable)** - Parse XML string → table\n2. **xml.decompile(adrTable)** - Serialize table → XML string\n\n**Complexity**: LOW - Just expose existing `xmlcompile()` and `xmldecompile()`\n\n**Dependencies**: None\n\n**Implementation**:\n```c\ncase xmlv_compile: {\n    Handle htext;\n    tyvaluerecord valtable;\n    hdlhashtable ht;\n    bigstring bs;\n    xmladdress xmladr;\n\n    // Get XML string parameter\n    if (!getstringvalue(hparam1, 1, htext))\n        return false;\n\n    // Get address parameter for result table\n    flnextparamislast = true;\n    if (!getaddressparam(hparam1, 2, &valtable))\n        return false;\n\n    if (!getaddressvalue(valtable, &ht, bs))\n        return false;\n\n    xmladr.ht = ht;\n    copystring(bs, xmladr.bs);\n\n    // Call existing parser\n    if (!xmlcompile(htext, &xmladr))\n        return false;\n\n    return setbooleanvalue(true, vreturned);\n}\n```\n\n**Test Cases** (6 tests):\n- Simple element compile/decompile\n- Nested elements\n- Attributes\n- CDATA sections\n- Empty tags\n- Round-trip (compile → decompile → compile)\n\n---\n\n### Phase 2: Tree Navigation (Medium Priority)\n**Goal**: Query and traverse parsed XML trees\n\n**Verbs**:\n3. **xml.getAddress(adrTable, name)** - Find first element\n4. **xml.getAddressList(adrTable, name)** - Find all elements\n5. **xml.convertToDisplayName(name)** - Strip `\"0001\\t\"` prefix\n\n**Complexity**: LOW - Expose existing `xmlgetaddress()`, `xmlgetaddresslist()`, `xmlgetname()`\n\n**Dependencies**: Phase 1 (need xml.compile to create test data)\n\n**Implementation**:\n```c\ncase xmlv_getaddress: {\n    hdlhashtable ht;\n    bigstring bs, name;\n    tyvaluerecord valtable;\n\n    // Get table address\n    if (!getaddressparam(hparam1, 1, &valtable))\n        return false;\n\n    if (!getaddressvalue(valtable, &ht, bs))\n        return false;\n\n    // Get element name to find\n    flnextparamislast = true;\n    if (!getstringvalue(hparam1, 2, name))\n        return false;\n\n    // Call existing function (modifies ht, bs in-place to found element)\n    if (!xmlgetaddress(ht, name))\n        return false;\n\n    // Return address of found element\n    return setaddressvalue(ht, bs, vreturned);\n}\n```\n\n**Test Cases** (8 tests):\n- Find single element\n- Find nested element\n- Find non-existent element (should error)\n- Find multiple elements (getAddressList)\n- Strip serial prefix (convertToDisplayName)\n- Empty table\n- Case sensitivity\n\n---\n\n### Phase 3: Attribute Access (Medium Priority)\n**Goal**: Read XML attributes\n\n**Verbs**:\n6. **xml.getAttribute(adrTable, name)** - Get `/atts` sub-table\n7. **xml.getAttributeValue(adrTable, name)** - Get attribute value directly\n\n**Complexity**: MEDIUM - Needs wrapper around `/atts` table lookup\n\n**Dependencies**: Phase 2 (uses getAddress internally)\n\n**Implementation**:\n```c\ncase xmlv_getattribute: {\n    hdlhashtable ht, htatts;\n    bigstring bs, name;\n    tyvaluerecord valtable;\n\n    // Get element address\n    if (!getaddressparam(hparam1, 1, &valtable))\n        return false;\n\n    if (!getaddressvalue(valtable, &ht, bs))\n        return false;\n\n    // Get attribute name\n    flnextparamislast = true;\n    if (!getstringvalue(hparam1, 2, name))\n        return false;\n\n    // Call existing function (from langxml.h)\n    if (!xmlgetattribute(ht, name, &htatts))\n        return false;\n\n    // Return address of /atts table\n    return setaddressvalue(htatts, name, vreturned);\n}\n```\n\n**Test Cases** (6 tests):\n- Read single attribute\n- Read multiple attributes\n- Read non-existent attribute (should error)\n- Attributes with special characters\n- Empty attributes table\n\n---\n\n### Phase 4: Tree Building (Medium Priority)\n**Goal**: Programmatically construct XML trees\n\n**Verbs**:\n8. **xml.addTable(adrParent, name)** - Create sub-table element\n9. **xml.addValue(adrParent, name, value)** - Create value element\n\n**Complexity**: MEDIUM - Needs serial number generation\n\n**Dependencies**: Phase 1 (for testing with decompile)\n\n**Implementation**:\n```c\ncase xmlv_addtable: {\n    hdlhashtable ht, htnew;\n    bigstring bs, name;\n    tyvaluerecord valtable;\n    xmladdress adr;\n\n    // Get parent table address\n    if (!getaddressparam(hparam1, 1, &valtable))\n        return false;\n\n    if (!getaddressvalue(valtable, &ht, bs))\n        return false;\n\n    // Get element name\n    flnextparamislast = true;\n    if (!getstringvalue(hparam1, 2, name))\n        return false;\n\n    // Generate serialized name and create sub-table\n    getnewitemaddress(ht, name, &adr);\n\n    if (!langassignnewtablevalue(adr.ht, adr.bs, &htnew))\n        return false;\n\n    // Return address of new table\n    return setaddressvalue(htnew, bs, vreturned);\n}\n```\n\n**Test Cases** (8 tests):\n- Add table to root\n- Add nested tables\n- Add multiple tables with same name (serial numbering)\n- Add value elements\n- Build complex tree from scratch\n- Decompile built tree to verify structure\n\n---\n\n### Phase 5: Type Conversions (Low Priority)\n**Goal**: Convert between XML-RPC and Frontier types\n\n**Verbs**:\n10. **xml.valToString(val, indentLevel)** - Scalar → XML-RPC format\n11. **xml.structtofrontiervalue(adrstruct, adrFrontierVal)** - XML struct → Frontier value\n\n**Complexity**: LOW - Expose existing `xmlvaltostring()` and `xmlstructtofrontiervalue()`\n\n**Dependencies**: Phase 1 (for test data)\n\n**Notes**:\n- `xml.valToString` may be duplicate of `xml.frontiervaluetotaggedtext` (already implemented)\n- Need to verify if they're the same or have subtle differences\n- `xmlstructtofrontiervalue()` already exists at line 703\n\n**Implementation**:\n```c\ncase xmlv_valtostring: {\n    tyvaluerecord val;\n    long indentlevel;\n    Handle htext;\n\n    // Get value parameter (not address - direct value)\n    if (!getparamvalue(hparam1, 1, &val))\n        return false;\n\n    // Get indent level\n    flnextparamislast = true;\n    if (!getlongvalue(hparam1, 2, &indentlevel))\n        return false;\n\n    // Call existing function\n    if (!xmlvaltostring(val, indentlevel, true, &htext))\n        return false;\n\n    return setheapvalue(htext, stringvaluetype, vreturned);\n}\n```\n\n**Test Cases** (10 tests):\n- Convert integers, booleans, strings, doubles\n- Convert dates (ISO8601 format)\n- Convert binary (base64)\n- Convert structs/arrays\n- Indent level parameter\n- structtofrontiervalue round-trip\n\n---\n\n## File Modifications\n\n### Files to Modify\n\n1. **tests/headless_xml_verbs.c** (180 lines)\n   - Change all stub cases to call existing langxml.c functions\n   - Add parameter extraction and result packing\n   - Estimated: +400 lines of implementation code\n\n2. **Common/headers/langxml.h** (50 lines)\n   - Verify all needed functions are exported\n   - May need to add exports for `xmlgetaddresslist`, `xmlgetattribute`, etc.\n   - Estimated: +10 lines\n\n3. **Common/source/langxml.c** (3600+ lines)\n   - **No major changes needed** - functions already exist!\n   - Possible minor fixes for headless build compatibility\n   - Estimated: +50 lines of fixes/adjustments\n\n4. **tests/integration/test_cases/xml_verbs.yaml** (352 lines)\n   - Add ~60 new integration tests (10 tests per phase)\n   - Estimated: +1200 lines\n\n### Files to Create\n\n5. **planning/phase3/XML_VERB_IMPLEMENTATION_PLAN.md** (this file)\n\n---\n\n## Implementation Phases (Detailed)\n\n### Phase 1: Core Parser/Serializer (Week 1)\n\n**Goal**: Basic XML ↔ Table conversion\n\n**Verbs**: xml.compile, xml.decompile\n\n**Tasks**:\n1. ✅ Read existing `xmlcompile()` implementation (line 1834)\n2. Run tests\n\n**Success Criteria**: 44 tests passing (34 from Phase 1-4 + 10 new)\n\n**Estimated Effort**: 6-8 hours\n\n---\n\n## Testing Strategy\n\n### Test-Driven Development Approach\n\nFor each verb:\n1. **Write integration test first** (in `tests/integration/test_cases/xml_verbs.yaml`)\n2. **Run test** (should fail with \"not implemented\")\n3. **Implement verb** (minimal code to pass test)\n4. **Run test** (should pass)\n5. **Refactor** (if needed)\n6. **Add edge case tests**\n7. **Repeat**\n\n### Integration Test Template\n\n```yaml\n- name: \"xml.compile - simple element\"\n  script: |\n    local(s = \"<name>John</name>\");\n    xml.compile(s, @scratchpad.test);\n    local(result = defined(scratchpad.test[\"0001\\tname\"]));\n    delete(@scratchpad.test);\n    return result\n  expected_output: \"true\"\n  expected_success: true\n\n- name: \"xml.decompile - simple element\"\n  script: |\n    new(tableType, @scratchpad.test);\n    new(tableType, @scratchpad.test[\"0001\\tname\"]);\n    scratchpad.test[\"0001\\tname\"][\"/contents\"] = \"John\";\n    local(result = xml.decompile(@scratchpad.test));\n    delete(@scratchpad.test);\n    return result\n  expected_output: \"<name>John</name>\"\n  expected_success: true\n```\n\n### Unit Test Coverage\n\nNo C unit tests needed - all functions already exist and are tested through:\n1. OPML import/export (uses xml.compile/decompile internally)\n2. XML-RPC calls (uses xmlfrontiervaltotaggedtext)\n3. Integration tests (comprehensive UserTalk-level testing)\n\n### Test Execution\n\n```bash\n# Run all integration tests\ncd tests && make test-integration\n\n# Run only xml tests\ncd tests && python3 run_integration_tests.py --filter xml\n\n# Run specific test\ncd tests && python3 run_integration_tests.py --filter \"xml.compile - simple element\"\n```\n\n---\n\n## Risk Assessment\n\n### Low Risk Areas ✅\n\n1. **Parser Implementation** - Already exists and tested (OPML uses it)\n2. **Serializer Implementation** - Already exists and tested\n3. **Basic Wrappers** - Simple parameter extraction → function call → return value\n\n### Medium Risk Areas ⚠️\n\n1. **Headless Build Compatibility**\n   - **Risk**: Some functions may depend on GUI-only features\n   - **Mitigation**: langxml.c line 1875 already has `#ifdef FRONTIER_HEADLESS` guard\n   - **Action**: Add similar guards if needed\n\n2. **Memory Management**\n   - **Risk**: Handle ownership between C parser and UserTalk\n   - **Mitigation**: Follow existing patterns from xml.frontiervaluetotaggedtext\n   - **Action**: Use `setheapvalue()` to transfer handle ownership\n\n3. **Error Handling**\n   - **Risk**: Parser errors may use `scriptError()` which isn't headless-friendly\n   - **Mitigation**: Parser already handles headless (line 1875)\n   - **Action**: Wrap parser calls in error checking\n\n### High Risk Areas 🔴\n\n1. **Serial Number Generation** (`serialstring()` line 1253)\n   - **Risk**: Multiple verbs creating elements simultaneously could conflict\n   - **Mitigation**: Serial numbers use 8 digits with gaps (e.g., 1000, 2000, 3000)\n   - **Action**: Test concurrent creation scenarios\n\n2. **Table Ownership** (External vs. In-Memory)\n   - **Risk**: xml.compile creates external table variables in headless mode (line 1877)\n   - **Mitigation**: Code already has headless path to use root table directly\n   - **Action**: Verify table lifecycle in all scenarios\n\n---\n\n## Implementation Gotchas\n\n### 1. Serialized Names\n\n**Problem**: Element names are stored as `\"0001\\tname\"`, not `\"name\"`\n\n**Solution**: Always use helper functions:\n- `xmlgetname()` - Strip prefix\n- `isxmlmatch()` - Compare names (ignoring prefix)\n- `getnewitemaddress()` - Create new serialized entry\n\n### 2. Attribute Storage\n\n**Problem**: Attributes are in a sub-table, not as direct properties\n\n**Solution**: Always access via `/atts` sub-table:\n```c\n// WRONG - won't find attribute\nhashtablelookup(ht, \"age\", &val, &hnode)\n\n// RIGHT - use attribute helper\nxmlgetattribute(ht, \"age\", &htatts)\nhashtablelookup(htatts, \"age\", &val, &hnode)\n```\n\n### 3. Text Content\n\n**Problem**: Element text is in `/contents`, `/pcdata`, or `/cdata` sub-items\n\n**Solution**: Check special keys:\n```c\n// Element with text: <name>John</name>\n// Stored as: table[\"0001\\tname\"][\"/contents\"] = \"John\"\n```\n\n### 4. Handle Ownership\n\n**Problem**: Who owns the Handle after returning from C to UserTalk?\n\n**Solution**: Use `setheapvalue()` to transfer ownership:\n```c\nHandle htext;\n// ... create htext ...\n\n// Transfer ownership to UserTalk\nreturn setheapvalue(htext, stringvaluetype, vreturned);\n// DON'T dispose htext - UserTalk owns it now!\n```\n\n### 5. Parameter Order\n\n**Problem**: Frontier parameters are 1-indexed, not 0-indexed\n\n**Solution**: Always use correct indices:\n```c\n// First parameter\ngetstringvalue(hparam1, 1, str)  // NOT 0\n\n// Last parameter\nflnextparamislast = true;\ngetstringvalue(hparam1, 2, str)\n```\n\n---\n\n## Success Metrics\n\n### Completion Criteria\n\n- ✅ All 13 verbs implemented\n- ✅ Minimum 44 integration tests passing\n- ✅ No memory leaks (tested with longer test runs)\n- ✅ Round-trip tests pass (compile → decompile → compile)\n- ✅ OPML import/export still works (regression test)\n- ✅ XML-RPC calls still work (regression test)\n\n### Performance Targets\n\n- xml.compile: < 100ms for typical OPML files (< 1000 elements)\n- xml.decompile: < 50ms for typical tables\n- xml.getAddress: < 1ms (hash table lookup)\n- xml.addTable: < 1ms (table creation + serial number)\n\n### Code Quality\n\n- No new compiler warnings\n- Follow existing code style in langxml.c\n- Use structured logging (`log_trace`, `log_error`) not `fprintf(stderr)`\n- Add comments explaining non-obvious logic\n- Update file headers to mark as production code (not stubs)\n\n---\n\n## Dependencies & Assumptions\n\n### Dependencies\n\n1. **Existing Code**:\n   - langxml.c (3600+ lines) - Parser and serializer\n   - opxml.c (2223 lines) - OPML-specific XML operations\n   - langxml.h - Function exports\n\n2. **Build System**:\n   - frontier-cli/Makefile - Already includes langxml.c\n   - No new dependencies needed\n\n3. **Testing Infrastructure**:\n   - Integration test framework (Python YAML runner)\n   - Test temp directory support\n   - OPML test files (for regression)\n\n### Assumptions\n\n1. **Parser is Correct**: Assume existing xmlcompile() is bug-free (proven by OPML usage)\n2. **Serializer is Correct**: Assume existing xmldecompile() works (proven by OPML export)\n3. **Headless Support**: Assume existing `#ifdef FRONTIER_HEADLESS` guards are sufficient\n4. **Memory Safety**: Assume existing handle management patterns are safe\n5. **Thread Safety**: Assume xml parser is single-threaded (like rest of UserTalk runtime)\n\n---\n\n## Timeline Estimate\n\n### Optimistic (Full-Time Focus)\n- **Week 1**: Phase 1 + Phase 2 (20 tests)\n- **Week 2**: Phase 3 + Phase 4 (14 tests)\n- **Week 3**: Phase 5 + Polish (10 tests + docs)\n- **Total**: 3 weeks\n\n### Realistic (Part-Time, 10-15 hours/week)\n- **Week 1-2**: Phase 1 (12 tests)\n- **Week 3-4**: Phase 2 + Phase 3 (14 tests)\n- **Week 5-6**: Phase 4 (8 tests)\n- **Week 7**: Phase 5 + Polish (10 tests + docs)\n- **Total**: 6-7 weeks\n\n### Conservative (With Interruptions, Unknown Issues)\n- **Week 1-3**: Phase 1 + debugging\n- **Week 4-6**: Phase 2 + Phase 3\n- **Week 7-9**: Phase 4\n- **Week 10-12**: Phase 5 + comprehensive testing\n- **Total**: 12 weeks\n\n**Recommended**: Plan for **realistic timeline (6-7 weeks)** with buffer for unknowns.\n\n---\n\n## Open Questions\n\n### 1. xml.valToString vs xml.frontiervaluetotaggedtext\n\n**Question**: Are these the same function or different?\n\n**Investigation Needed**:\n- Compare output format for same input\n- Check if one handles indenting differently\n- Verify parameter signatures match\n\n**Resolution Plan**:\n- Read both implementations side-by-side\n- Run test cases through both\n- If identical: make one call the other\n- If different: document the difference\n\n### 2. {"parentUuid":"daa25845-683b-42b2-9818-492ef02d6b14","isSidechain":true,"userType":"external","cwd":"/Users/jake/dev/jsavin/Frontier","sessionId":"fb8cbe5a-3375-43a0-8935-a2a6c9ce6050","version":"2.1.3","gitBranch":"develop","agentId":"acc0d99","slug":"curious-twirling-sifakis","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01SdekHsz83R8Ya2h1Syryrm","type":"tool_result","content":"File created successfully at: /Users/jake/dev/jsavin/Frontier/planning/phase3/XML_VERB_IMPLEMENTATION_PLAN.md"}]},"uuid":"6ef8a5f4-2f32-4f94-a52d-0ee5ae57a35f","timestamp":"2026-01-13T01:31:42.164Z","toolUseResult":{"type":"create","filePath":"/Users/jake/dev/jsavin/Frontier/planning/phase3/XML_VERB_IMPLEMENTATION_PLAN.md","content":"# XML Verb Implementation Plan\n\n**Status**: Ready for Implementation\n**Created**: 2026-01-12\n**Target Branch**: `feature/xml-verbs`\n**Approach**: Test-Driven Development (TDD)\n\n## Executive Summary\n\nFrontier has a custom XML parser (not libxml2) that converts XML strings into a hierarchical database table structure. The parser is already implemented in `Common/source/langxml.c` with 1,834+ lines of existing code. This plan implements the 13 remaining XML kernel verbs by exposing this existing parser infrastructure through the UserTalk API.\n\n**Key Insight**: Most implementation work is **wrapping existing C functions** in the verb dispatcher, not writing new parsers from scratch.\n\n---\n\n## Current Status\n\n### Implemented (2/18 total verbs)\n1. ✅ **xml.frontiervaluetotaggedtext** - C implementation (37 integration tests passing)\n2. ✅ **xml.getPathAddress** - Pure UserTalk implementation (no kernel stub needed)\n\n### Pure UserTalk (4/18 - no kernel implementation needed)\n3. ✅ **xml.entityDecode** - UserTalk only\n4. ✅ **xml.entityEncode** - UserTalk only\n5. ✅ **xml.convertRssFolderToOpml** - UserTalk only\n6. ✅ **xml.rss.flRssItem** - UserTalk only\n\n### To Implement (13/18 remaining kernel verbs)\n- **xml.compile** - Parser (XML string → database table)\n- **xml.decompile** - Serializer (database table → XML string)\n- **xml.valToString** - Scalar → XML-RPC format\n- **xml.structtofrontiervalue** - XML struct → Frontier value\n- **xml.getAddress** - Find first element by name\n- **xml.getAddressList** - Find all elements with same name\n- **xml.getAttribute** - Get attribute by name\n- **xml.getAttributeValue** - Get attribute value\n- **xml.addTable** - Create sub-table element\n- **xml.addValue** - Create value element\n- **xml.convertToDisplayName** - Strip internal encoding from names\n- **xml.frontiervaluetotaggedtext** - (duplicate/related to valToString?)\n\n---\n\n## XML Structure in Frontier's Database\n\n### Serialized Naming Convention\n\nXML elements are stored with **serialized numeric prefixes** to preserve order:\n\n```\n\"0001\\titemName\"    // First item named \"itemName\"\n\"0002\\titemName\"    // Second item with same name\n\"00001000\\tfoo\"     // Serial numbers can have gaps\n```\n\n**Format**: `{8-digit-number}\\t{element-name}`\n\n**Why**: Hash tables don't preserve insertion order, so serial numbers maintain document order.\n\n### Special Sub-Tables\n\n- **`/atts`** - Attributes table (holds XML attributes)\n- **`/contents`** - Text content (for elements with text)\n- **`/pcdata`** - Parsed character data (text nodes)\n- **`/cdata`** - CDATA sections\n- **`/comment`** - XML comments (not usually compiled)\n- **`/doctype`** - DOCTYPE declarations\n- **`/pi`** - Processing instructions\n\n### Example Structure\n\n**XML Input**:\n```xml\n<person age=\"30\">\n  <name>John</name>\n  <city>Boston</city>\n</person>\n```\n\n**Database Table Structure**:\n```\ntable {\n  \"0001\\tperson\" {\n    \"/atts\" {\n      \"age\" = \"30\"\n    }\n    \"0001\\tname\" {\n      \"/contents\" = \"John\"\n    }\n    \"0002\\tcity\" {\n      \"/contents\" = \"Boston\"\n    }\n  }\n}\n```\n\n---\n\n## Existing Parser Infrastructure (Already Implemented)\n\n### Core Parser Functions (langxml.c)\n\n| Function | Line | Purpose |\n|----------|------|---------|\n| `xmlcompile()` | 1834 | **Main parser** - XML string → table structure |\n| `xmldecompile()` | 2869 | **Serializer** - table structure → XML string |\n| `xmlgetaddress()` | 2962 | Find first element by name |\n| `xmlgetaddresslist()` | 2986 | Find all elements with same name |\n| `xmlgetname()` | 2238 | Strip serial prefix from name |\n| `isxmlmatch()` | 2938 | Match element by name (ignoring serial) |\n| `xmlgetattribute()` | (header) | Get attribute table |\n| `xmlfrontiervaltotaggedtext()` | 380 | Frontier value → XML-RPC (already exposed) |\n| `xmlstructtofrontiervalue()` | 703 | XML struct → Frontier value |\n\n### Tokenizer Functions (langxml.c)\n\n- `getnexttoken()` - Lexical analyzer\n- `newxmltoken()` / `disposexmltoken()` - Token lifecycle\n- `assignxmltoken()` - Token assignment\n- `createtokentable()` - Create table from token\n- `serialstring()` - Generate serial numbers\n- `getnewitemaddress()` - Create serialized table entry\n\n### Helper Functions\n\n- `replaceallinhandle()` - Entity encoding/decoding\n- `handlebeginswith()` / `handlecontains()` - String matching\n- `trimtrailingwhitespace()` - Whitespace normalization\n- `breakatfirstwhitespacechar()` - Tag parsing\n\n---\n\n## Implementation Strategy\n\n### Phase 1: Core Parser/Serializer (Highest Priority)\n**Goal**: Enable XML → Table → XML round-trip\n\n**Verbs**:\n1. **xml.compile(s, adrTable)** - Parse XML string → table\n2. **xml.decompile(adrTable)** - Serialize table → XML string\n\n**Complexity**: LOW - Just expose existing `xmlcompile()` and `xmldecompile()`\n\n**Dependencies**: None\n\n**Implementation**:\n```c\ncase xmlv_compile: {\n    Handle htext;\n    tyvaluerecord valtable;\n    hdlhashtable ht;\n    bigstring bs;\n    xmladdress xmladr;\n\n    // Get XML string parameter\n    if (!getstringvalue(hparam1, 1, htext))\n        return false;\n\n    // Get address parameter for result table\n    flnextparamislast = true;\n    if (!getaddressparam(hparam1, 2, &valtable))\n        return false;\n\n    if (!getaddressvalue(valtable, &ht, bs))\n        return false;\n\n    xmladr.ht = ht;\n    copystring(bs, xmladr.bs);\n\n    // Call existing parser\n    if (!xmlcompile(htext, &xmladr))\n        return false;\n\n    return setbooleanvalue(true, vreturned);\n}\n```\n\n**Test Cases** (6 tests):\n- Simple element compile/decompile\n- Nested elements\n- Attributes\n- CDATA sections\n- Empty tags\n- Round-trip (compile → decompile → compile)\n\n---\n\n### Phase 2: Tree Navigation (Medium Priority)\n**Goal**: Query and traverse parsed XML trees\n\n**Verbs**:\n3. **xml.getAddress(adrTable, name)** - Find first element\n4. **xml.getAddressList(adrTable, name)** - Find all elements\n5. **xml.convertToDisplayName(name)** - Strip `\"0001\\t\"` prefix\n\n**Complexity**: LOW - Expose existing `xmlgetaddress()`, `xmlgetaddresslist()`, `xmlgetname()`\n\n**Dependencies**: Phase 1 (need xml.compile to create test data)\n\n**Implementation**:\n```c\ncase xmlv_getaddress: {\n    hdlhashtable ht;\n    bigstring bs, name;\n    tyvaluerecord valtable;\n\n    // Get table address\n    if (!getaddressparam(hparam1, 1, &valtable))\n        return false;\n\n    if (!getaddressvalue(valtable, &ht, bs))\n        return false;\n\n    // Get element name to find\n    flnextparamislast = true;\n    if (!getstringvalue(hparam1, 2, name))\n        return false;\n\n    // Call existing function (modifies ht, bs in-place to found element)\n    if (!xmlgetaddress(ht, name))\n        return false;\n\n    // Return address of found element\n    return setaddressvalue(ht, bs, vreturned);\n}\n```\n\n**Test Cases** (8 tests):\n- Find single element\n- Find nested element\n- Find non-existent element (should error)\n- Find multiple elements (getAddressList)\n- Strip serial prefix (convertToDisplayName)\n- Empty table\n- Case sensitivity\n\n---\n\n### Phase 3: Attribute Access (Medium Priority)\n**Goal**: Read XML attributes\n\n**Verbs**:\n6. **xml.getAttribute(adrTable, name)** - Get `/atts` sub-table\n7. **xml.getAttributeValue(adrTable, name)** - Get attribute value directly\n\n**Complexity**: MEDIUM - Needs wrapper around `/atts` table lookup\n\n**Dependencies**: Phase 2 (uses getAddress internally)\n\n**Implementation**:\n```c\ncase xmlv_getattribute: {\n    hdlhashtable ht, htatts;\n    bigstring bs, name;\n    tyvaluerecord valtable;\n\n    // Get element address\n    if (!getaddressparam(hparam1, 1, &valtable))\n        return false;\n\n    if (!getaddressvalue(valtable, &ht, bs))\n        return false;\n\n    // Get attribute name\n    flnextparamislast = true;\n    if (!getstringvalue(hparam1, 2, name))\n        return false;\n\n    // Call existing function (from langxml.h)\n    if (!xmlgetattribute(ht, name, &htatts))\n        return false;\n\n    // Return address of /atts table\n    return setaddressvalue(htatts, name, vreturned);\n}\n```\n\n**Test Cases** (6 tests):\n- Read single attribute\n- Read multiple attributes\n- Read non-existent attribute (should error)\n- Attributes with special characters\n- Empty attributes table\n\n---\n\n### Phase 4: Tree Building (Medium Priority)\n**Goal**: Programmatically construct XML trees\n\n**Verbs**:\n8. **xml.addTable(adrParent, name)** - Create sub-table element\n9. **xml.addValue(adrParent, name, value)** - Create value element\n\n**Complexity**: MEDIUM - Needs serial number generation\n\n**Dependencies**: Phase 1 (for testing with decompile)\n\n**Implementation**:\n```c\ncase xmlv_addtable: {\n    hdlhashtable ht, htnew;\n    bigstring bs, name;\n    tyvaluerecord valtable;\n    xmladdress adr;\n\n    // Get parent table address\n    if (!getaddressparam(hparam1, 1, &valtable))\n        return false;\n\n    if (!getaddressvalue(valtable, &ht, bs))\n        return false;\n\n    // Get element name\n    flnextparamislast = true;\n    if (!getstringvalue(hparam1, 2, name))\n        return false;\n\n    // Generate serialized name and create sub-table\n    getnewitemaddress(ht, name, &adr);\n\n    if (!langassignnewtablevalue(adr.ht, adr.bs, &htnew))\n        return false;\n\n    // Return address of new table\n    return setaddressvalue(htnew, bs, vreturned);\n}\n```\n\n**Test Cases** (8 tests):\n- Add table to root\n- Add nested tables\n- Add multiple tables with same name (serial numbering)\n- Add value elements\n- Build complex tree from scratch\n- Decompile built tree to verify structure\n\n---\n\n### Phase 5: Type Conversions (Low Priority)\n**Goal**: Convert between XML-RPC and Frontier types\n\n**Verbs**:\n10. **xml.valToString(val, indentLevel)** - Scalar → XML-RPC format\n11. **xml.structtofrontiervalue(adrstruct, adrFrontierVal)** - XML struct → Frontier value\n\n**Complexity**: LOW - Expose existing `xmlvaltostring()` and `xmlstructtofrontiervalue()`\n\n**Dependencies**: Phase 1 (for test data)\n\n**Notes**:\n- `xml.valToString` may be duplicate of `xml.frontiervaluetotaggedtext` (already implemented)\n- Need to verify if they're the same or have subtle differences\n- `xmlstructtofrontiervalue()` already exists at line 703\n\n**Implementation**:\n```c\ncase xmlv_valtostring: {\n    tyvaluerecord val;\n    long indentlevel;\n    Handle htext;\n\n    // Get value parameter (not address - direct value)\n    if (!getparamvalue(hparam1, 1, &val))\n        return false;\n\n    // Get indent level\n    flnextparamislast = true;\n    if (!getlongvalue(hparam1, 2, &indentlevel))\n        return false;\n\n    // Call existing function\n    if (!xmlvaltostring(val, indentlevel, true, &htext))\n        return false;\n\n    return setheapvalue(htext, stringvaluetype, vreturned);\n}\n```\n\n**Test Cases** (10 tests):\n- Convert integers, booleans, strings, doubles\n- Convert dates (ISO8601 format)\n- Convert binary (base64)\n- Convert structs/arrays\n- Indent level parameter\n- structtofrontiervalue round-trip\n\n---\n\n## File Modifications\n\n### Files to Modify\n\n1. **tests/headless_xml_verbs.c** (180 lines)\n   - Change all stub cases to call existing langxml.c functions\n   - Add parameter extraction and result packing\n   - Estimated: +400 lines of implementation code\n\n2. **Common/headers/langxml.h** (50 lines)\n   - Verify all needed functions are exported\n   - May need to add exports for `xmlgetaddresslist`, `xmlgetattribute`, etc.\n   - Estimated: +10 lines\n\n3. **Common/source/langxml.c** (3600+ lines)\n   - **No major changes needed** - functions already exist!\n   - Possible minor fixes for headless build compatibility\n   - Estimated: +50 lines of fixes/adjustments\n\n4. **tests/integration/test_cases/xml_verbs.yaml** (352 lines)\n   - Add ~60 new integration tests (10 tests per phase)\n   - Estimated: +1200 lines\n\n### Files to Create\n\n5. **planning/phase3/XML_VERB_IMPLEMENTATION_PLAN.md** (this file)\n\n---\n\n## Implementation Phases (Detailed)\n\n### Phase 1: Core Parser/Serializer (Week 1)\n\n**Goal**: Basic XML ↔ Table conversion\n\n**Verbs**: xml.compile, xml.decompile\n\n**Tasks**:\n1. ✅ Read existing `xmlcompile()` implementation (line 1834)\n2. Write integration tests for xml.compile\n   - Simple element: `<name>John</name>`\n   - Nested elements: `<person><name>John</name></person>`\n   - Attributes: `<person age=\"30\">John</person>`\n   - CDATA: `<![CDATA[<script>alert(1)</script>]]>`\n   - Empty tags: `<br/>`\n   - Processing instructions: `<?xml version=\"1.0\"?>`\n3. Implement xml.compile verb wrapper\n4. Write integration tests for xml.decompile\n   - Round-trip tests (compile → decompile → compare)\n   - Table → XML with proper formatting\n   - Preserve attribute order\n5. Implement xml.decompile verb wrapper\n6. Run tests: `cd tests && make test-integration`\n\n**Success Criteria**: 12 tests passing\n\n**Estimated Effort**: 8-12 hours\n\n---\n\n### Phase 2: Tree Navigation (Week 1-2)\n\n**Goal**: Query parsed XML structures\n\n**Verbs**: xml.getAddress, xml.getAddressList, xml.convertToDisplayName\n\n**Tasks**:\n1. Write tests for xml.getAddress\n   - Find root element\n   - Find nested element\n   - Find non-existent element (scriptError expected)\n2. Implement xml.getAddress wrapper (calls `xmlgetaddress()` line 2962)\n3. Write tests for xml.getAddressList\n   - Find multiple elements with same name\n   - Return list of addresses\n   - Empty results\n4. Implement xml.getAddressList wrapper (calls `xmlgetaddresslist()` line 2986)\n5. Write tests for xml.convertToDisplayName\n   - Strip `\"0001\\t\"` prefix\n   - Handle names without prefix\n6. Run tests\n\n**Success Criteria**: 20 tests passing (12 from Phase 1 + 8 new)\n\n**Estimated Effort**: 6-8 hours\n\n---\n\n### Phase 3: Attribute Access (Week 2)\n\n**Goal**: Read XML attributes\n\n**Verbs**: xml.getAttribute, xml.getAttributeValue\n\n**Tasks**:\n1. Write tests for xml.getAttribute\n   - Read `/atts` sub-table\n   - Multiple attributes\n   - No attributes (empty table or error?)\n2. Implement xml.getAttribute wrapper\n3. Write tests for xml.getAttributeValue\n   - Direct value access (convenience wrapper)\n   - Non-existent attributes\n4. Implement xml.getAttributeValue wrapper\n5. Run tests\n\n**Success Criteria**: 26 tests passing (20 from Phase 1+2 + 6 new)\n\n**Estimated Effort**: 4-6 hours\n\n---\n\n### Phase 4: Tree Building (Week 2-3)\n\n**Goal**: Programmatically create XML structures\n\n**Verbs**: xml.addTable, xml.addValue\n\n**Tasks**:\n1. Study serial number generation (`serialstring()` line 1253)\n2. Write tests for xml.addTable\n   - Create root table\n   - Create nested tables\n   - Multiple tables with same name (serial numbers)\n3. Implement xml.addTable wrapper\n4. Write tests for xml.addValue\n   - Add string values\n   - Add numeric values\n   - Add to `/contents` or `/pcdata`\n5. Implement xml.addValue wrapper\n6. Write tests for complete tree building\n   - Build complex structure\n   - Decompile to verify XML output\n7. Run tests\n\n**Success Criteria**: 34 tests passing (26 from Phase 1+2+3 + 8 new)\n\n**Estimated Effort**: 8-10 hours\n\n---\n\n### Phase 5: Type Conversions (Week 3)\n\n**Goal**: XML-RPC type conversions\n\n**Verbs**: xml.valToString, xml.structtofrontiervalue\n\n**Tasks**:\n1. Investigate relationship between `valToString` and `frontiervaluetotaggedtext`\n   - Check if they're duplicates\n   - Identify any behavioral differences\n2. If different, write tests for xml.valToString\n   - Scalars: int, bool, double, string, date\n   - Collections: lists, tables\n   - Indent levels\n3. Implement xml.valToString wrapper (if needed)\n4. Write tests for xml.structtofrontiervalue\n   - XML struct → Frontier table\n   - XML array → Frontier list\n   - XML base64 → binary\n   - Round-trip conversions\n5. Implement xml.structtofrontiervalue wrapper (calls line 703)\n6. Run tests\n\n**Success Criteria**: 44 tests passing (34 from Phase 1-4 + 10 new)\n\n**Estimated Effort**: 6-8 hours\n\n---\n\n## Testing Strategy\n\n### Test-Driven Development Approach\n\nFor each verb:\n1. **Write integration test first** (in `tests/integration/test_cases/xml_verbs.yaml`)\n2. **Run test** (should fail with \"not implemented\")\n3. **Implement verb** (minimal code to pass test)\n4. **Run test** (should pass)\n5. **Refactor** (if needed)\n6. **Add edge case tests**\n7. **Repeat**\n\n### Integration Test Template\n\n```yaml\n- name: \"xml.compile - simple element\"\n  script: |\n    local(s = \"<name>John</name>\");\n    xml.compile(s, @scratchpad.test);\n    local(result = defined(scratchpad.test[\"0001\\tname\"]));\n    delete(@scratchpad.test);\n    return result\n  expected_output: \"true\"\n  expected_success: true\n\n- name: \"xml.decompile - simple element\"\n  script: |\n    new(tableType, @scratchpad.test);\n    new(tableType, @scratchpad.test[\"0001\\tname\"]);\n    scratchpad.test[\"0001\\tname\"][\"/contents\"] = \"John\";\n    local(result = xml.decompile(@scratchpad.test));\n    delete(@scratchpad.test);\n    return result\n  expected_output: \"<name>John</name>\"\n  expected_success: true\n```\n\n### Unit Test Coverage\n\nNo C unit tests needed - all functions already exist and are tested through:\n1. OPML import/export (uses xml.compile/decompile internally)\n2. XML-RPC calls (uses xmlfrontiervaltotaggedtext)\n3. Integration tests (comprehensive UserTalk-level testing)\n\n### Test Execution\n\n```bash\n# Run all integration tests\ncd tests && make test-integration\n\n# Run only xml tests\ncd tests && python3 run_integration_tests.py --filter xml\n\n# Run specific test\ncd tests && python3 run_integration_tests.py --filter \"xml.compile - simple element\"\n```\n\n---\n\n## Risk Assessment\n\n### Low Risk Areas ✅\n\n1. **Parser Implementation** - Already exists and tested (OPML uses it)\n2. **Serializer Implementation** - Already exists and tested\n3. **Basic Wrappers** - Simple parameter extraction → function call → return value\n\n### Medium Risk Areas ⚠️\n\n1. **Headless Build Compatibility**\n   - **Risk**: Some functions may depend on GUI-only features\n   - **Mitigation**: langxml.c line 1875 already has `#ifdef FRONTIER_HEADLESS` guard\n   - **Action**: Add similar guards if needed\n\n2. **Memory Management**\n   - **Risk**: Handle ownership between C parser and UserTalk\n   - **Mitigation**: Follow existing patterns from xml.frontiervaluetotaggedtext\n   - **Action**: Use `setheapvalue()` to transfer handle ownership\n\n3. **Error Handling**\n   - **Risk**: Parser errors may use `scriptError()` which isn't headless-friendly\n   - **Mitigation**: Parser already handles headless (line 1875)\n   - **Action**: Wrap parser calls in error checking\n\n### High Risk Areas 🔴\n\n1. **Serial Number Generation** (`serialstring()` line 1253)\n   - **Risk**: Multiple verbs creating elements simultaneously could conflict\n   - **Mitigation**: Serial numbers use 8 digits with gaps (e.g., 1000, 2000, 3000)\n   - **Action**: Test concurrent creation scenarios\n\n2. **Table Ownership** (External vs. In-Memory)\n   - **Risk**: xml.compile creates external table variables in headless mode (line 1877)\n   - **Mitigation**: Code already has headless path to use root table directly\n   - **Action**: Verify table lifecycle in all scenarios\n\n---\n\n## Implementation Gotchas\n\n### 1. Serialized Names\n\n**Problem**: Element names are stored as `\"0001\\tname\"`, not `\"name\"`\n\n**Solution**: Always use helper functions:\n- `xmlgetname()` - Strip prefix\n- `isxmlmatch()` - Compare names (ignoring prefix)\n- `getnewitemaddress()` - Create new serialized entry\n\n### 2. Attribute Storage\n\n**Problem**: Attributes are in a sub-table, not as direct properties\n\n**Solution**: Always access via `/atts` sub-table:\n```c\n// WRONG - won't find attribute\nhashtablelookup(ht, \"age\", &val, &hnode)\n\n// RIGHT - use attribute helper\nxmlgetattribute(ht, \"age\", &htatts)\nhashtablelookup(htatts, \"age\", &val, &hnode)\n```\n\n### 3. Text Content\n\n**Problem**: Element text is in `/contents`, `/pcdata`, or `/cdata` sub-items\n\n**Solution**: Check special keys:\n```c\n// Element with text: <name>John</name>\n// Stored as: table[\"0001\\tname\"][\"/contents\"] = \"John\"\n```\n\n### 4. Handle Ownership\n\n**Problem**: Who owns the Handle after returning from C to UserTalk?\n\n**Solution**: Use `setheapvalue()` to transfer ownership:\n```c\nHandle htext;\n// ... create htext ...\n\n// Transfer ownership to UserTalk\nreturn setheapvalue(htext, stringvaluetype, vreturned);\n// DON'T dispose htext - UserTalk owns it now!\n```\n\n### 5. Parameter Order\n\n**Problem**: Frontier parameters are 1-indexed, not 0-indexed\n\n**Solution**: Always use correct indices:\n```c\n// First parameter\ngetstringvalue(hparam1, 1, str)  // NOT 0\n\n// Last parameter\nflnextparamislast = true;\ngetstringvalue(hparam1, 2, str)\n```\n\n---\n\n## Success Metrics\n\n### Completion Criteria\n\n- ✅ All 13 verbs implemented\n- ✅ Minimum 44 integration tests passing\n- ✅ No memory leaks (tested with longer test runs)\n- ✅ Round-trip tests pass (compile → decompile → compile)\n- ✅ OPML import/export still works (regression test)\n- ✅ XML-RPC calls still work (regression test)\n\n### Performance Targets\n\n- xml.compile: < 100ms for typical OPML files (< 1000 elements)\n- xml.decompile: < 50ms for typical tables\n- xml.getAddress: < 1ms (hash table lookup)\n- xml.addTable: < 1ms (table creation + serial number)\n\n### Code Quality\n\n- No new compiler warnings\n- Follow existing code style in langxml.c\n- Use structured logging (`log_trace`, `log_error`) not `fprintf(stderr)`\n- Add comments explaining non-obvious logic\n- Update file headers to mark as production code (not stubs)\n\n---\n\n## Dependencies & Assumptions\n\n### Dependencies\n\n1. **Existing Code**:\n   - langxml.c (3600+ lines) - Parser and serializer\n   - opxml.c (2223 lines) - OPML-specific XML operations\n   - langxml.h - Function exports\n\n2. **Build System**:\n   - frontier-cli/Makefile - Already includes langxml.c\n   -
